========== FILE: ./src/App.tsx ==========

import React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from 'react';
import {
  INITIAL_PATIENT,
  INITIAL_EVENTS,
  INITIAL_DOCUMENTS,
  INITIAL_SHARING_SESSIONS,
  INITIAL_AUDIT_LOGS,
} from './data/mockData';
import {
  PatientProfile,
  HealthEvent,
  MedicalDocument,
  SharingSession,
  AuditLogEntry,
  SharingScope,
} from './types';
import { api } from './services/api';
import { Navbar } from './components/layout/Navbar';
import { Sidebar, TabType } from './components/layout/Sidebar';
import { DashboardView } from './components/views/DashboardView';
import { JournalView } from './components/views/JournalView';
import { VaultView } from './components/views/VaultView';
import { SharingView } from './components/views/SharingView';
import { AuditLogView } from './components/views/AuditLogView';
import { DoctorPortalView } from './components/views/DoctorPortalView';
import { EmergencyModal } from './components/modals/EmergencyModal';
import { EventFormModal } from './components/modals/EventFormModal';
import { DocumentUploadModal } from './components/modals/DocumentUploadModal';
import { DocumentPreviewModal } from './components/modals/DocumentPreviewModal';
import { ToastContainer, ToastMessage } from './components/common/Toast';

export const App: React.FC = () => {
  // Main states
  const [patient, setPatient] = useState<PatientProfile>(INITIAL_PATIENT);
  const [events, setEvents] = useState<HealthEvent[]>(INITIAL_EVENTS);
  const [documents, setDocuments] = useState<MedicalDocument[]>(INITIAL_DOCUMENTS);
  const [sharingSessions, setSharingSessions] = useState<SharingSession[]>(INITIAL_SHARING_SESSIONS);
  const [auditLogs, setAuditLogs] = useState<AuditLogEntry[]>(INITIAL_AUDIT_LOGS);

  // UI state
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<TabType | 'doctor'>('dashboard');
  const [doctorToken, setDoctorToken] = useState<string | undefined>(undefined);
  const [isMobileMenuOpen, setIsMobileMenuOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isBackendConnected, setIsBackendConnected] = useState(false);

  // Modals state
  const [isEmergencyOpen, setIsEmergencyOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isEventModalOpen, setIsEventModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [eventToEdit, setEventToEdit] = useState<HealthEvent | null>(null);
  const [isUploadModalOpen, setIsUploadModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [previewDocument, setPreviewDocument] = useState<MedicalDocument | null>(null);

  // Toasts
  const [toasts, setToasts] = useState<ToastMessage[]>([]);

  const addToast = (type: ToastMessage['type'], title: string, description?: string) => {
    const id = `toast_${Date.now()}_${Math.random()}`;
    setToasts((prev) => [...prev, { id, type, title, description }]);
    setTimeout(() => {
      setToasts((prev) => prev.filter((t) => t.id !== id));
    }, 4500);
  };

  const removeToast = (id: string) => {
    setToasts((prev) => prev.filter((t) => t.id !== id));
  };

  // Check URL pathname for /share/:token on mount
  useEffect(() => {
    const path = window.location.pathname;
    if (path.startsWith('/share/')) {
      const token = path.replace('/share/', '').trim();
      if (token) {
        setDoctorToken(token);
        setActiveTab('doctor');
      }
    }
  }, []);

  // Sync data with Rust backend API if reachable
  const loadBackendData = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const isAlive = await api.checkHealth();
      setIsBackendConnected(isAlive);

      if (isAlive) {
        const [pat, evts, docs, shares, logs] = await Promise.all([
          api.getPatient().catch(() => INITIAL_PATIENT),
          api.getEvents().catch(() => INITIAL_EVENTS),
          api.getDocuments().catch(() => INITIAL_DOCUMENTS),
          api.getSharingSessions().catch(() => INITIAL_SHARING_SESSIONS),
          api.getAuditLogs().catch(() => INITIAL_AUDIT_LOGS),
        ]);

        if (pat) setPatient(pat);
        if (evts && evts.length > 0) setEvents(evts);
        if (docs && docs.length > 0) setDocuments(docs);
        if (shares && shares.length > 0) setSharingSessions(shares);
        if (logs && logs.length > 0) setAuditLogs(logs);
      }
    } catch {
      setIsBackendConnected(false);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadBackendData();
    const interval = setInterval(loadBackendData, 15000);
    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, [loadBackendData]);

  // Event Handlers
  const handleSaveEvent = async (
    eventData: Omit<HealthEvent, 'id' | 'created_at'>,
    existingId?: string
  ) => {
    if (existingId) {
      // Update
      const updatedList = events.map((e) =>
        e.id === existingId
          ? { ...e, ...eventData, updated_at: new Date().toISOString() }
          : e
      );
      setEvents(updatedList);
      addToast('success', 'Événement modifié', `"${eventData.title}" a été mis à jour.`);

      // Log in audit
      const auditEntry: AuditLogEntry = {
        id: `log_${Date.now()}`,
        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
        action: 'RECORD_UPDATED',
        actor: 'Yassine Mansouri (Patient)',
        actor_role: 'PATIENT',
        ip_address: '127.0.0.1 (Local)',
        details: `Modification de l'événement médical : ${eventData.title}`,
        status: 'SUCCESS',
      };
      setAuditLogs((prev) => [auditEntry, ...prev]);

      if (isBackendConnected) {
        api.updateEvent(existingId, eventData).catch(console.error);
      }
    } else {
      // Create new
      const newId = `evt_${Date.now()}`;
      const newEvent: HealthEvent = {
        ...eventData,
        id: newId,
        created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
      };
      setEvents([newEvent, ...events]);
      addToast('success', 'Événement ajouté', `"${eventData.title}" a été enregistré au carnet.`);

      // Log in audit
      const auditEntry: AuditLogEntry = {
        id: `log_${Date.now()}`,
        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
        action: 'RECORD_CREATED',
        actor: 'Yassine Mansouri (Patient)',
        actor_role: 'PATIENT',
        ip_address: '127.0.0.1 (Local)',
        details: `Ajout au carnet de santé : ${eventData.title} (${eventData.category})`,
        status: 'SUCCESS',
      };
      setAuditLogs((prev) => [auditEntry, ...prev]);

      if (isBackendConnected) {
        api.createEvent(eventData).catch(console.error);
      }
    }
    setEventToEdit(null);
  };

  const handleDeleteEvent = async (id: string) => {
    const deleted = events.find((e) => e.id === id);
    setEvents((prev) => prev.filter((e) => e.id !== id));
    addToast('info', 'Événement supprimé', deleted ? `"${deleted.title}" a été retiré.` : undefined);

    if (deleted) {
      const auditEntry: AuditLogEntry = {
        id: `log_${Date.now()}`,
        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
        action: 'RECORD_DELETED',
        actor: 'Yassine Mansouri (Patient)',
        actor_role: 'PATIENT',
        ip_address: '127.0.0.1 (Local)',
        details: `Suppression de l'événement médical : ${deleted.title}`,
        status: 'WARNING',
      };
      setAuditLogs((prev) => [auditEntry, ...prev]);
    }

    if (isBackendConnected) {
      api.deleteEvent(id).catch(console.error);
    }
  };

  const handleUploadDocument = async (docData: Omit<MedicalDocument, 'id' | 'created_at'>) => {
    const newId = `doc_${Date.now()}`;
    const newDoc: MedicalDocument = {
      ...docData,
      id: newId,
      created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
    };
    setDocuments([newDoc, ...documents]);
    addToast('success', 'Document certifié ajouté', `"${docData.title}" a été scellé dans le coffre-fort.`);

    const auditEntry: AuditLogEntry = {
      id: `log_${Date.now()}`,
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
      action: 'DOCUMENT_UPLOADED',
      actor: 'Yassine Mansouri (Patient)',
      actor_role: 'PATIENT',
      ip_address: '127.0.0.1 (Local)',
      details: `Dépôt sécurisé au coffre-fort : ${docData.title} (${(docData.file_size_bytes / 1024).toFixed(1)} Ko)`,
      status: 'SUCCESS',
    };
    setAuditLogs((prev) => [auditEntry, ...prev]);

    if (isBackendConnected) {
      api.createDocument(docData).catch(console.error);
    }
  };

  const handleDeleteDocument = async (id: string) => {
    const doc = documents.find((d) => d.id === id);
    setDocuments((prev) => prev.filter((d) => d.id !== id));
    addToast('info', 'Document supprimé', doc ? `"${doc.title}" a été supprimé.` : undefined);

    if (doc) {
      const auditEntry: AuditLogEntry = {
        id: `log_${Date.now()}`,
        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
        action: 'DOCUMENT_DELETED',
        actor: 'Yassine Mansouri (Patient)',
        actor_role: 'PATIENT',
        ip_address: '127.0.0.1 (Local)',
        details: `Suppression du document du coffre-fort : ${doc.title}`,
        status: 'WARNING',
      };
      setAuditLogs((prev) => [auditEntry, ...prev]);
    }

    if (isBackendConnected) {
      api.deleteDocument(id).catch(console.error);
    }
  };

  const handleCreateSharingSession = async (
    recipientLabel: string,
    purpose: string,
    durationMinutes: number,
    oneTimeUse: boolean,
    scope: SharingScope
  ) => {
    // Generate unguessable random token
    const randomHex = Array.from(crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(8)))
      .map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0'))
      .join('');
    const token = `carni-sec-${randomHex}`;
    const newSession: SharingSession = {
      id: `sh_${Date.now()}`,
      token,
      recipient_label: recipientLabel,
      purpose,
      scope,
      expires_at: new Date(Date.now() + durationMinutes * 60 * 1000).toISOString(),
      created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
      is_revoked: false,
      access_count: 0,
      one_time_use: oneTimeUse,
    };

    setSharingSessions([newSession, ...sharingSessions]);
    addToast(
      'success',
      'QR Code de partage créé !',
      `Session active pour ${recipientLabel} (${durationMinutes} min).`
    );

    const auditEntry: AuditLogEntry = {
      id: `log_${Date.now()}`,
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
      action: 'SESSION_CREATED',
      actor: 'Yassine Mansouri (Patient)',
      actor_role: 'PATIENT',
      ip_address: '127.0.0.1 (Local)',
      details: `Création session QR Code (${durationMinutes} min) pour ${recipientLabel} • Scope: ${scope.all_events ? 'Tous les événements' : `${scope.event_ids.length} événements`}, ${scope.all_docs ? 'Tous les docs' : `${scope.doc_ids.length} docs`}`,
      status: 'SUCCESS',
    };
    setAuditLogs((prev) => [auditEntry, ...prev]);

    if (isBackendConnected) {
      api.createSharingSession({
        recipient_label: recipientLabel,
        purpose,
        duration_minutes: durationMinutes,
        one_time_use: oneTimeUse,
        scope,
      }).catch(console.error);
    }
  };

  const handleRevokeSession = async (sessionId: string) => {
    const session = sharingSessions.find((s) => s.id === sessionId);
    setSharingSessions((prev) =>
      prev.map((s) => (s.id === sessionId ? { ...s, is_revoked: true } : s))
    );
    addToast('info', 'Accès révoqué', 'Le jeton de partage a été invalidé immédiatement.');

    if (session) {
      const auditEntry: AuditLogEntry = {
        id: `log_${Date.now()}`,
        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
        action: 'SESSION_REVOKED',
        actor: 'Yassine Mansouri (Patient)',
        actor_role: 'PATIENT',
        ip_address: '127.0.0.1 (Local)',
        details: `Révocation manuelle immédiate de l'accès pour ${session.recipient_label} (token: ${session.token})`,
        status: 'REVOKED',
      };
      setAuditLogs((prev) => [auditEntry, ...prev]);
    }

    if (isBackendConnected) {
      api.revokeSharingSession(sessionId).catch(console.error);
    }
  };

  const handleOpenDoctorPortal = (token?: string) => {
    setDoctorToken(token);
    setActiveTab('doctor');
  };

  // If viewing Doctor Portal
  if (activeTab === 'doctor') {
    return (
      <DoctorPortalView
        token={doctorToken}
        patient={patient}
        events={events}
        documents={documents}
        sessions={sharingSessions}
        onBackToApp={() => setActiveTab('dashboard')}
        onPreviewDocument={(doc) => setPreviewDocument(doc)}
      />
    );
  }

  const activeShareCount = sharingSessions.filter(
    (s) => !s.is_revoked && new Date(s.expires_at).getTime() > Date.now()
  ).length;

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 flex flex-col font-['Plus_Jakarta_Sans',sans-serif]">
      {/* Top Navbar */}
      <Navbar
        patient={patient}
        onOpenEmergency={() => setIsEmergencyOpen(true)}
        onToggleMobileMenu={() => setIsMobileMenuOpen(!isMobileMenuOpen)}
        onOpenDoctorPortal={() => handleOpenDoctorPortal()}
        isBackendConnected={isBackendConnected}
      />

      {/* Main Layout */}
      <div className="flex-1 flex max-w-7xl w-full mx-auto">
        <Sidebar
          activeTab={activeTab}
          onSelectTab={(tab) => setActiveTab(tab)}
          isOpenMobile={isMobileMenuOpen}
          onCloseMobile={() => setIsMobileMenuOpen(false)}
          eventCount={events.length}
          docCount={documents.length}
          activeShareCount={activeShareCount}
        />

        {/* Main Content Area */}
        <main className="flex-1 p-4 sm:p-6 lg:p-8 min-w-0">
          {activeTab === 'dashboard' && (
            <DashboardView
              patient={patient}
              events={events}
              documents={documents}
              sharingSessions={sharingSessions}
              onNavigate={(tab) => setActiveTab(tab)}
              onOpenNewEvent={() => {
                setEventToEdit(null);
                setIsEventModalOpen(true);
              }}
              onOpenUploadDoc={() => setIsUploadModalOpen(true)}
              onOpenEmergency={() => setIsEmergencyOpen(true)}
              onOpenDoctorPortal={(token) => handleOpenDoctorPortal(token)}
              onRevokeSession={handleRevokeSession}
            />
          )}

          {activeTab === 'journal' && (
            <JournalView
              events={events}
              documents={documents}
              onOpenNewEvent={() => {
                setEventToEdit(null);
                setIsEventModalOpen(true);
              }}
              onEditEvent={(event) => {
                setEventToEdit(event);
                setIsEventModalOpen(true);
              }}
              onDeleteEvent={handleDeleteEvent}
              onPreviewDocument={(doc) => setPreviewDocument(doc)}
            />
          )}

          {activeTab === 'vault' && (
            <VaultView
              documents={documents}
              onOpenUpload={() => setIsUploadModalOpen(true)}
              onPreviewDocument={(doc) => setPreviewDocument(doc)}
              onDeleteDocument={handleDeleteDocument}
            />
          )}

          {activeTab === 'sharing' && (
            <SharingView
              patient={patient}
              events={events}
              documents={documents}
              sessions={sharingSessions}
              onCreateSession={handleCreateSharingSession}
              onRevokeSession={handleRevokeSession}
              onOpenDoctorPortal={handleOpenDoctorPortal}
            />
          )}

          {activeTab === 'audit' && <AuditLogView logs={auditLogs} />}
        </main>
      </div>

      {/* Modals */}
      <EmergencyModal
        patient={patient}
        isOpen={isEmergencyOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsEmergencyOpen(false)}
      />

      <EventFormModal
        isOpen={isEventModalOpen}
        onClose={() => {
          setIsEventModalOpen(false);
          setEventToEdit(null);
        }}
        onSave={handleSaveEvent}
        eventToEdit={eventToEdit}
        documents={documents}
      />

      <DocumentUploadModal
        isOpen={isUploadModalOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsUploadModalOpen(false)}
        onUpload={handleUploadDocument}
      />

      <DocumentPreviewModal
        document={previewDocument}
        isOpen={!!previewDocument}
        onClose={() => setPreviewDocument(null)}
      />

      {/* Toast notifications container */}
      <ToastContainer toasts={toasts} onDismiss={removeToast} />
    </div>
  );
};


========== FILE: ./src/components/common/QRCodeDisplay.tsx ==========

import React, { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { generateQRCodeMatrix } from '../../utils/qr';
import { Copy, Check, ExternalLink, ShieldAlert, Clock, Sparkles } from 'lucide-react';

interface QRCodeDisplayProps {
  value: string;
  size?: number;
  recipientLabel?: string;
  expiresAt?: string;
  isRevoked?: boolean;
  onOpenDoctorView?: () => void;
  onRevoke?: () => void;
}

export const QRCodeDisplay: React.FC<QRCodeDisplayProps> = ({
  value,
  size = 220,
  recipientLabel,
  expiresAt,
  isRevoked = false,
  onOpenDoctorView,
  onRevoke,
}) => {
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);

  const matrix = useMemo(() => {
    try {
      return generateQRCodeMatrix(value);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('QR generation error:', err);
      return [];
    }
  }, [value]);

  const copyToClipboard = () => {
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(value);
    setCopied(true);
    setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2000);
  };

  const isExpired = expiresAt ? new Date(expiresAt).getTime() < Date.now() : false;
  const isInactive = isRevoked || isExpired;

  const moduleCount = matrix.length;
  const cellSize = moduleCount > 0 ? size / (moduleCount + 4) : 10;
  const offset = cellSize * 2;

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col items-center bg-white rounded-2xl p-6 border border-slate-200/80 shadow-sm relative overflow-hidden">
      {/* Background glow */}
      <div className="absolute -top-16 -right-16 w-36 h-36 bg-teal-500/10 rounded-full blur-2xl pointer-events-none" />

      {/* Status banner if inactive */}
      {isRevoked && (
        <div className="w-full mb-4 px-3 py-2 bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 rounded-lg flex items-center gap-2 text-rose-700 text-xs font-semibold">
          <ShieldAlert className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" />
          <span>Accès révoqué définitivement par le patient</span>
        </div>
      )}
      {isExpired && !isRevoked && (
        <div className="w-full mb-4 px-3 py-2 bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 rounded-lg flex items-center gap-2 text-amber-700 text-xs font-semibold">
          <Clock className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" />
          <span>Session expirée (délai dépassé)</span>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* QR Code Container */}
      <div className={`relative p-3 rounded-xl bg-white border border-slate-100 shadow-inner ${isInactive ? 'opacity-40 grayscale' : ''}`}>
        {matrix.length > 0 ? (
          <svg
            width={size}
            height={size}
            viewBox={`0 0 ${size} ${size}`}
            className="shape-rendering-crispEdges block"
          >
            <rect width={size} height={size} fill="#ffffff" rx={8} />
            {matrix.map((row, y) =>
              row.map((cell, x) =>
                cell ? (
                  <rect
                    key={`${x}-${y}`}
                    x={offset + x * cellSize}
                    y={offset + y * cellSize}
                    width={cellSize * 0.96}
                    height={cellSize * 0.96}
                    rx={cellSize * 0.2}
                    fill="#0f172a"
                  />
                ) : null
              )
            )}
            {/* Center security seal */}
            <circle
              cx={size / 2}
              cy={size / 2}
              r={size * 0.09}
              fill="#0d9488"
              stroke="#ffffff"
              strokeWidth={2.5}
            />
          </svg>
        ) : (
          <div className="flex items-center justify-center w-48 h-48 text-slate-400 text-xs">
            Génération du code...
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {recipientLabel && (
        <div className="mt-3 text-center">
          <span className="text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wider font-semibold text-slate-400 block">
            Destinataire autorisé
          </span>
          <span className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-800">
            {recipientLabel}
          </span>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Secret token badge */}
      <div className="mt-3 w-full bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200/60 rounded-lg p-2.5 flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
          <div className="text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-slate-400">Jeton de partage sécurisé</div>
          <div className="text-xs font-mono font-medium text-teal-800 truncate">
            {value}
          </div>
        </div>
        <button
          onClick={copyToClipboard}
          className="shrink-0 p-1.5 hover:bg-slate-200/70 text-slate-600 rounded-md transition-colors"
          title="Copier le lien d'accès"
        >
          {copied ? <Check className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-600" /> : <Copy className="w-4 h-4" />}
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Actions */}
      <div className="mt-4 w-full flex items-center gap-2">
        {onOpenDoctorView && (
          <button
            onClick={onOpenDoctorView}
            className="flex-1 py-2 px-3 bg-teal-600 hover:bg-teal-700 text-white text-xs font-semibold rounded-lg shadow-sm flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 transition-colors"
          >
            <ExternalLink className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            <span>Tester vue médecin</span>
          </button>
        )}
        {onRevoke && !isInactive && (
          <button
            onClick={onRevoke}
            className="py-2 px-3 bg-rose-50 hover:bg-rose-100 text-rose-700 border border-rose-200 text-xs font-semibold rounded-lg transition-colors flex items-center gap-1"
          >
            <ShieldAlert className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            <span>Révoquer</span>
          </button>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="mt-3 flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] text-slate-400">
        <Sparkles className="w-3 h-3 text-teal-500" />
        <span>Chiffré de bout en bout • Zéro donnée dans le QR code</span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};


========== FILE: ./src/components/common/Toast.tsx ==========

import React from 'react';
import { CheckCircle2, AlertCircle, Info, X } from 'lucide-react';

export interface ToastMessage {
  id: string;
  type: 'success' | 'error' | 'info';
  title: string;
  description?: string;
}

interface ToastContainerProps {
  toasts: ToastMessage[];
  onDismiss: (id: string) => void;
}

export const ToastContainer: React.FC<ToastContainerProps> = ({ toasts, onDismiss }) => {
  if (toasts.length === 0) return null;

  return (
    <div className="fixed bottom-5 right-5 z-50 flex flex-col gap-2 max-w-sm w-full pointer-events-none">
      {toasts.map((toast) => (
        <div
          key={toast.id}
          className={`pointer-events-auto flex items-start gap-3 p-4 rounded-xl shadow-lg border backdrop-blur-md transition-all duration-300 animate-in slide-in-from-bottom-3 ${
            toast.type === 'success'
              ? 'bg-white/95 border-emerald-200 text-slate-800'
              : toast.type === 'error'
              ? 'bg-white/95 border-rose-200 text-slate-800'
              : 'bg-white/95 border-teal-200 text-slate-800'
          }`}
        >
          {toast.type === 'success' && <CheckCircle2 className="w-5 h-5 text-emerald-600 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />}
          {toast.type === 'error' && <AlertCircle className="w-5 h-5 text-rose-600 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />}
          {toast.type === 'info' && <Info className="w-5 h-5 text-teal-600 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />}

          <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
            <h4 className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900">{toast.title}</h4>
            {toast.description && (
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-600 mt-0.5 line-clamp-2">{toast.description}</p>
            )}
          </div>

          <button
            onClick={() => onDismiss(toast.id)}
            className="text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600 p-1 -mr-1 -mt-1 rounded-md"
          >
            <X className="w-4 h-4" />
          </button>
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
};


========== FILE: ./src/components/layout/Navbar.tsx ==========

interface NavbarProps {

  onOpenEmergency: () => void;
  onToggleMobileMenu: () => void;

}

export const Navbar: React.FC<NavbarProps> = ({
  patient,
  onOpenEmergency,
  onToggleMobileMenu,
  onOpenDoctorPortal,
  isBackendConnected,
}) => {
  return (
    <header className="sticky top-0 z-30 bg-white/90 backdrop-blur-md border-b border-slate-200/80 px-4 sm:px-6 py-3">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between max-w-7xl mx-auto">
        {/* Brand & Mobile Hamburger */}

            className="md:hidden p-2 rounded-xl text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100"

            <Menu className="w-5 h-5" />
          </button>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
            <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-br from-teal-500 to-teal-700 text-white flex items-center justify-center shadow-md shadow-teal-500/20 font-black text-lg tracking-wider">
              C
            </div>
            <div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <span className="font-extrabold text-lg text-slate-900 tracking-tight">
                  CARNI
                </span>
                <span className="text-[10px] font-bold px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-teal-50 text-teal-700 border border-teal-200">
                  MAROC
                </span>
              </div>
              <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 -mt-1 hidden sm:block">
                Carnet de Santé Numérique & Souverain

              isBackendConnected
                ? 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border-emerald-200'
                : 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border-amber-200'

            title={isBackendConnected ? 'Connecté à l\'API Axum Rust (SQLite)' : 'Mode local / Démo'}
          >
            {isBackendConnected ? (
              <>
                <Wifi className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-600" />

            ) : (
              <>
                <WifiOff className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-600" />
                <span className="font-medium">Mode Local Actif</span>
              </>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Doctor Portal View Direct Button */}
          <button
            onClick={onOpenDoctorPortal}
            className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl border border-teal-200 bg-teal-50/70 hover:bg-teal-100/80 text-teal-800 text-xs font-semibold flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-colors"
          >
            <Stethoscope className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-teal-600" />
            <span className="hidden md:inline">Espace Médecin</span>
          </button>

          {/* Emergency Pill with live pulse */}
          <button
            onClick={onOpenEmergency}
            className="relative px-3 sm:px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-700 text-white text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 shadow-sm shadow-rose-600/30 transition-all hover:scale-[1.02] active:scale-[0.98]"
          >
            <span className="relative flex h-2 w-2">
              <span className="animate-ping absolute inline-flex h-full w-full rounded-full bg-white opacity-75"></span>
              <span className="relative inline-flex rounded-full h-2 w-2 bg-white"></span>
            </span>
            <AlertTriangle className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            <span>Fiche Urgence</span>
          </button>

          {/* Patient Quick Chip */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 pl-2 border-l border-slate-200">
            <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-slate-900 text-white flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-xs shadow-xs">
              YM
            </div>
            <div className="hidden sm:block text-left">
              <div className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-800 leading-tight">
                {patient.full_name}
              </div>
              <div className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-mono">
                {patient.cin_cnss}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </header>
  );
};


========== FILE: ./src/components/layout/Sidebar.tsx ==========

import React from 'react';
import {
  LayoutDashboard,
  BookOpen,
  FolderLock,
  QrCode,
  ShieldCheck,
  History,
  X,
  HeartPulse,
  Info,
} from 'lucide-react';

export type TabType = 'dashboard' | 'journal' | 'vault' | 'sharing' | 'audit';

interface SidebarProps {
  activeTab: TabType;
  onSelectTab: (tab: TabType) => void;
  isOpenMobile: boolean;
  onCloseMobile: () => void;
  eventCount: number;
  docCount: number;
  activeShareCount: number;
}

export const Sidebar: React.FC<SidebarProps> = ({
  activeTab,
  onSelectTab,
  isOpenMobile,
  onCloseMobile,
  eventCount,
  docCount,
  activeShareCount,
}) => {
  const navItems = [
    {
      id: 'dashboard' as TabType,
      label: 'Vue d\'ensemble',
      icon: LayoutDashboard,
      badge: null,
    },
    {
      id: 'journal' as TabType,
      label: 'Carnet de Santé',
      icon: BookOpen,
      badge: eventCount > 0 ? eventCount : null,
    },
    {
      id: 'vault' as TabType,
      label: 'Coffre-fort Médical',
      icon: FolderLock,
      badge: docCount > 0 ? docCount : null,
    },
    {
      id: 'sharing' as TabType,
      label: 'Partage Sécurisé & QR',
      icon: QrCode,
      badge: activeShareCount > 0 ? `${activeShareCount} actif` : null,
      badgeColor: activeShareCount > 0 ? 'bg-teal-500 text-white' : undefined,
    },
    {
      id: 'audit' as TabType,
      label: 'Journal d\'Audit',
      icon: History,
      badge: null,
    },
  ];

  const handleNavClick = (tab: TabType) => {
    onSelectTab(tab);
    if (isOpenMobile) onCloseMobile();
  };

  const content = (
    <div className="flex flex-col h-full bg-white border-r border-slate-200/80 p-4">
      {/* Mobile close button */}
      <div className="md:hidden flex items-center justify-between pb-4 border-b border-slate-100 mb-4">
        <span className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400">Navigation</span>
        <button
          onClick={onCloseMobile}
          className="p-1 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-100 text-slate-500"
        >
          <X className="w-5 h-5" />
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Main Navigation links */}
      <div className="space-y-1.5 flex-1">
        <div className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider px-3 mb-2">
          Menu Principal
        </div>
        {navItems.map((item) => {
          const Icon = item.icon;
          const isActive = activeTab === item.id;
          return (
            <button
              key={item.id}
              onClick={() => handleNavClick(item.id)}
              className={`w-full flex items-center justify-between px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold transition-all ${
                isActive
                  ? 'bg-slate-900 text-white shadow-sm'
                  : 'text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100 hover:text-slate-900'
              }`}
            >
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <Icon
                  className={`w-4 h-4 ${
                    isActive ? 'text-teal-400' : 'text-slate-400 group-hover:text-slate-600'
                  }`}
                />
                <span>{item.label}</span>
              </div>
              {item.badge !== null && (
                <span
                  className={`text-[10px] px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full font-mono font-bold ${
                    item.badgeColor || (isActive ? 'bg-slate-800 text-teal-300' : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-600')
                  }`}
                >
                  {item.badge}
                </span>
              )}
            </button>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      {/* Security banner card */}
      <div className="p-3.5 bg-gradient-to-br from-teal-50 to-emerald-50/50 rounded-2xl border border-teal-100 text-xs text-teal-900 mt-4">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 font-bold text-teal-800 mb-1">
          <ShieldCheck className="w-4 h-4 text-teal-600" />
          <span>Souveraineté des Données</span>
        </div>
        <p className="text-[11px] text-teal-700/90 leading-relaxed">
          Vos données restent sous votre contrôle exclusif. Aucun partage n'a lieu sans votre consentement explicite.
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* Hackathon Disclaimer */}
      <div className="mt-4 pt-3 border-t border-slate-100 text-center">
        <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 block font-medium">
          CARNI MVP • Hackathon Santé Maroc 🇲🇦
        </span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );

  return (
    <>
      {/* Desktop Sidebar */}
      <aside className="hidden md:block w-64 shrink-0 h-[calc(100vh-61px)] sticky top-[61px]">
        {content}
      </aside>

      {/* Mobile Drawer Overlay */}
      {isOpenMobile && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-40 md:hidden bg-slate-900/50 backdrop-blur-xs flex">
          <div className="w-72 max-w-[85vw] h-full shadow-2xl animate-in slide-in-from-left duration-200">
            {content}
          </div>
          <div className="flex-1" onClick={onCloseMobile} />
        </div>
      )}
    </>
  );
};


========== FILE: ./src/components/modals/DocumentPreviewModal.tsx ==========

import React from 'react';
import { X, FileText, Download, Printer, Shield, Calendar, Building, Tag } from 'lucide-react';
import { MedicalDocument } from '../../types';

interface DocumentPreviewModalProps {
  document: MedicalDocument | null;
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
}

export const DocumentPreviewModal: React.FC<DocumentPreviewModalProps> = ({
  document,
  isOpen,
  onClose,
}) => {
  if (!isOpen || !document) return null;

  const formatFileSize = (bytes: number) => {
    if (bytes < 1024) return `${bytes} B`;
    if (bytes < 1024 * 1024) return `${(bytes / 1024).toFixed(1)} Ko`;
    return `${(bytes / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(2)} Mo`;
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-slate-900/60 backdrop-blur-sm animate-in fade-in duration-200">
      <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl max-w-2xl w-full shadow-2xl border border-slate-200 overflow-hidden flex flex-col max-h-[90vh]">
        {/* Modal Top Bar */}
        <div className="bg-slate-900 px-6 py-4 text-white flex items-center justify-between">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-teal-500/20 text-teal-400 border border-teal-500/30 flex items-center justify-center">
              <FileText className="w-5 h-5" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <span className="text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-teal-500/20 text-teal-300 uppercase tracking-wider">
                  {document.category}
                </span>
                <span className="text-xs text-slate-400 font-mono">
                  {formatFileSize(document.file_size_bytes)}
                </span>
              </div>
              <h2 className="text-base font-bold text-white truncate max-w-md">
                {document.title}
              </h2>
            </div>
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={onClose}
            className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-white/10 hover:bg-white/20 flex items-center justify-center transition-colors text-white"
          >
            <X className="w-5 h-5" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Document Meta Row */}
        <div className="bg-slate-50 px-6 py-2.5 border-b border-slate-200 text-xs text-slate-600 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
            <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <Building className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400" />
              <strong>{document.doctor_or_lab}</strong>
            </span>
            <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <Calendar className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400" />
              <span>{document.issue_date}</span>
            </span>
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-teal-700 font-medium">
            <Shield className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            <span>Document certifié CARNI Vault</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Document Body (Simulated Medical PDF Paper View) */}
        <div className="p-6 overflow-y-auto flex-1 bg-slate-100/70">
          <div className="bg-white p-8 rounded-xl shadow-xs border border-slate-200 min-h-[380px] font-sans relative">
            {/* Watermark */}
            <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center pointer-events-none opacity-5">
              <span className="text-6xl font-black text-slate-900 uppercase rotate-[-25deg]">
                CARNI VAULT
              </span>
            </div>

            {/* Document Content Header */}
            <div className="border-b-2 border-teal-800 pb-4 mb-6 flex items-start justify-between">
              <div>
                <h3 className="text-sm font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-teal-900">
                  {document.doctor_or_lab}
                </h3>
                <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">Royaume du Maroc • Système de Santé Numérique</p>
                <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 font-mono mt-0.5">Réf: {document.id.toUpperCase()}-2026</p>
              </div>
              <div className="text-right">
                <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase block">Date d'édition</span>
                <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-800">{document.issue_date}</span>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Structured Text Content Preview */}
            <div className="text-xs leading-relaxed text-slate-700 whitespace-pre-line font-mono bg-slate-50/60 p-4 rounded-lg border border-slate-100">
              {document.content_preview ||
                `Document médical numérisé : ${document.title}\nÉmis par : ${document.doctor_or_lab}\nFichier source : ${document.file_name}\nFormat : ${document.file_type}`}
            </div>

            {/* Document Notes */}
            {document.notes && (
              <div className="mt-4 p-3 bg-teal-50/60 border-l-3 border-teal-600 rounded-r-lg text-xs text-teal-900">
                <strong className="font-semibold">Note d'accompagnement :</strong> {document.notes}
              </div>
            )}

            {/* Tags */}
            {document.tags.length > 0 && (
              <div className="mt-4 flex items-center gap-1.5 flex-wrap">
                <Tag className="w-3 h-3 text-slate-400" />
                {document.tags.map((tag, i) => (
                  <span
                    key={i}
                    className="px-2 py-0.5 bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 rounded text-[10px] font-medium"
                  >
                    #{tag}
                  </span>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}

            {/* Doctor Signature Block */}
            <div className="mt-8 pt-4 border-t border-slate-100 flex items-center justify-between text-xs text-slate-500">
              <div>
                <span>Signature électronique certifiée</span>
                <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-400">Authentification SHA-256 valide</p>
              </div>
              <div className="text-right">
                <span className="font-semibold text-slate-800">{document.doctor_or_lab}</span>
                <p className="text-[10px] text-teal-600 font-bold uppercase">Cachet Numérique Apposé</p>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Footer Actions */}
        <div className="p-4 bg-white border-t border-slate-100 flex items-center justify-between">
          <span className="text-xs text-slate-400 font-mono">
            {document.file_name}
          </span>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <button
              onClick={() => window.print()}
              className="px-3.5 py-2 border border-slate-200 text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-100 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-colors"
            >
              <Printer className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-500" />
              <span>Imprimer</span>
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => {
                const element = window.document.createElement('a');
                const file = new Blob([document.content_preview || 'Document CARNI'], { type: 'text/plain' });
                element.href = URL.createObjectURL(file);
                element.download = document.file_name;
                window.document.body.appendChild(element);
                element.click();
                window.document.body.removeChild(element);
              }}
              className="px-4 py-2 bg-teal-600 hover:bg-teal-700 text-white rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-colors shadow-xs"
            >
              <Download className="w-4 h-4" />
              <span>Télécharger</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};


========== FILE: ./src/components/modals/DocumentUploadModal.tsx ==========

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { X, UploadCloud, FileText, AlertCircle, CheckCircle2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { MedicalDocument, DocumentCategory } from '../../types';

interface DocumentUploadModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onUpload: (doc: Omit<MedicalDocument, 'id' | 'created_at'>) => void;
}

export const DocumentUploadModal: React.FC<DocumentUploadModalProps> = ({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  onUpload,
}) => {
  const [title, setTitle] = useState('');
  const [category, setCategory] = useState<DocumentCategory>('ORDONNANCE');
  const [doctorOrLab, setDoctorOrLab] = useState('');
  const [issueDate, setIssueDate] = useState(new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10));
  const [notes, setNotes] = useState('');
  const [tagsStr, setTagsStr] = useState('');
  const [file, setFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [fileError, setFileError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [errors, setErrors] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const handleFileChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    setFileError(null);
    if (!e.target.files || e.target.files.length === 0) {
      setFile(null);
      return;
    }

    const selectedFile = e.target.files[0];
    const allowedTypes = ['application/pdf', 'image/jpeg', 'image/png'];
    const maxSizeBytes = 10 * 1024 * 1024; // 10MB

    if (!allowedTypes.includes(selectedFile.type)) {
      setFileError('Format non autorisé. Formats acceptés : PDF, JPG, PNG.');
      setFile(null);
      return;
    }

    if (selectedFile.size > maxSizeBytes) {
      setFileError('Fichier trop volumineux. Taille maximale autorisée : 10 Mo.');
      setFile(null);
      return;
    }

    setFile(selectedFile);
    if (!title.trim()) {
      // Auto-populate title from filename without extension
      const cleanName = selectedFile.name.replace(/\.[^/.]+$/, '').replace(/[-_]/g, ' ');
      setTitle(cleanName.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + cleanName.slice(1));
    }
  };

  const handleSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const newErrors: Record<string, string> = {};

    if (!title.trim()) newErrors.title = 'Le titre du document est obligatoire';
    if (!doctorOrLab.trim()) newErrors.doctorOrLab = 'Le médecin ou laboratoire émetteur est obligatoire';
    if (!issueDate) newErrors.issueDate = 'La date du document est obligatoire';
    if (!file) newErrors.file = 'Veuillez sélectionner un fichier PDF ou image';

    if (Object.keys(newErrors).length > 0) {
      setErrors(newErrors);
      return;
    }

    const tags = tagsStr
      .split(',')
      .map((t) => t.trim())
      .filter(Boolean);

    onUpload({
      title: title.trim(),
      category,
      file_name: file!.name,
      file_type: file!.type,
      file_size_bytes: file!.size,
      issue_date: issueDate,
      doctor_or_lab: doctorOrLab.trim(),
      notes: notes.trim() || undefined,
      content_preview: `DOCUMENT MÉDICAL CERTIFIÉ CARNI\n\nTitre : ${title.trim()}\nÉmetteur : ${doctorOrLab.trim()}\nDate : ${issueDate}\nFichier original : ${file!.name}\nTaille : ${(file!.size / 1024).toFixed(1)} Ko\n\nNotes : ${notes.trim() || 'Aucune note'}\nMots-clés : ${tags.join(', ') || 'Général'}`,
      tags,
    });

    onClose();
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-slate-900/60 backdrop-blur-sm animate-in fade-in duration-200">
      <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl max-w-xl w-full shadow-2xl border border-slate-200 overflow-hidden flex flex-col max-h-[90vh]">
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-slate-900 to-slate-800 px-6 py-4 text-white flex items-center justify-between">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-teal-500/20 text-teal-400 border border-teal-500/30 flex items-center justify-center">
              <UploadCloud className="w-5 h-5" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <span className="text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider font-bold text-teal-400">
                Coffre-fort Médical Sécurisé
              </span>
              <h2 className="text-base font-bold text-white">
                Ajouter un document médical certifié
              </h2>
            </div>
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={onClose}
            className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-white/10 hover:bg-white/20 flex items-center justify-center transition-colors text-white"
          >
            <X className="w-5 h-5" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Form Body */}
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="p-6 overflow-y-auto space-y-4 flex-1">
          {/* File Dropzone */}
          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
              Fichier médical (PDF ou Image) <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
            </label>
            <div className="relative border-2 border-dashed border-slate-300 hover:border-teal-500 rounded-2xl p-5 text-center transition-colors bg-slate-50/50">
              <input
                type="file"
                accept=".pdf,image/jpeg,image/png"
                onChange={handleFileChange}
                className="absolute inset-0 w-full h-full opacity-0 cursor-pointer"
              />
              {file ? (
                <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-3 text-left">
                  <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-lg bg-teal-100 text-teal-700 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
                    <CheckCircle2 className="w-6 h-6 text-teal-600" />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <p className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-800 truncate max-w-xs">{file.name}</p>
                    <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500">
                      {(file.size / 1024).toFixed(1)} Ko • {file.type || 'Fichier médical'}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="space-y-1">
                  <UploadCloud className="w-8 h-8 text-teal-600 mx-auto" />
                  <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700">
                    Cliquez ou glissez-déposez votre document ici
                  </p>
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">
                    PDF, JPG ou PNG • Taille max 10 Mo • Chiffrement local
                  </p>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
            {fileError && (
              <span className="text-[11px] text-rose-500 flex items-center gap-1 mt-1">
                <AlertCircle className="w-3 h-3" /> {fileError}
              </span>
            )}
            {errors.file && (
              <span className="text-[11px] text-rose-500 flex items-center gap-1 mt-1">
                <AlertCircle className="w-3 h-3" /> {errors.file}
              </span>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Title & Category */}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                Catégorie de document <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
              </label>
              <select
                value={category}
                onChange={(e) => setCategory(e.target.value as DocumentCategory)}
                className="w-full text-xs rounded-xl border border-slate-300 p-2.5 focus:border-teal-500 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-teal-500 bg-white"
              >
                <option value="ORDONNANCE">📄 Ordonnance Médicale</option>
                <option value="BILAN">🧪 Bilan Biologique / Sanguin</option>
                <option value="IMAGERIE">🩻 Radiologie & Scanner / IRM</option>
                <option value="COMPTE_RENDU">📑 Compte-Rendu Clinique</option>
                <option value="CERTIFICAT">📜 Certificat / Vaccination</option>
                <option value="AUTRE">📁 Autre document</option>
              </select>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                Date du document <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
              </label>
              <input
                type="date"
                value={issueDate}
                onChange={(e) => setIssueDate(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full text-xs rounded-xl border border-slate-300 p-2.5 focus:border-teal-500 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-teal-500"
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Document Title */}
          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
              Titre du document <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
            </label>
            <input
              type="text"
              placeholder="Ex: Ordonnance traitement asthme, Bilan métabolique Zerktouni..."
              value={title}
              onChange={(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)}
              className={`w-full text-xs rounded-xl border ${
                errors.title ? 'border-rose-400' : 'border-slate-300'
              } p-2.5 focus:border-teal-500 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-teal-500`}
            />
            {errors.title && (
              <span className="text-[11px] text-rose-500 flex items-center gap-1 mt-1">
                <AlertCircle className="w-3 h-3" /> {errors.title}
              </span>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Doctor or Lab */}
          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
              Médecin ou Laboratoire émetteur <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
            </label>
            <input
              type="text"
              placeholder="Ex: Laboratoire Zerktouni, Dr. Omar Bennani, Clinique Agdal..."
              value={doctorOrLab}
              onChange={(e) => setDoctorOrLab(e.target.value)}
              className={`w-full text-xs rounded-xl border ${
                errors.doctorOrLab ? 'border-rose-400' : 'border-slate-300'
              } p-2.5 focus:border-teal-500 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-teal-500`}
            />
            {errors.doctorOrLab && (
              <span className="text-[11px] text-rose-500 flex items-center gap-1 mt-1">
                <AlertCircle className="w-3 h-3" /> {errors.doctorOrLab}
              </span>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Tags */}
          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
              Mots-clés / Tags (séparés par des virgules)
            </label>
            <input
              type="text"
              placeholder="Ex: Asthme, Contrôle, Traitement, Urgent"
              value={tagsStr}
              onChange={(e) => setTagsStr(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full text-xs rounded-xl border border-slate-300 p-2.5 focus:border-teal-500 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-teal-500"
            />
          </div>

          {/* Notes */}
          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
              Notes personnelles (Optionnel)
            </label>
            <textarea
              rows={2}
              placeholder="Remarques, posologie mentionnée, recommandations..."
              value={notes}
              onChange={(e) => setNotes(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full text-xs rounded-xl border border-slate-300 p-2.5 focus:border-teal-500 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-teal-500"
            />
          </div>

          {/* Actions */}
          <div className="pt-2 flex items-center justify-end gap-2">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onClose}
              className="px-4 py-2 border border-slate-200 text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold transition-colors"
            >
              Annuler
            </button>
            <button
              type="submit"
              className="px-5 py-2 bg-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-800 text-white rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition-colors shadow-xs"
            >
              Chiffrer et enregistrer
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};


========== FILE: ./src/components/modals/EmergencyModal.tsx ==========

import React from 'react';
import { X, AlertTriangle, Phone, Heart, Activity, ShieldCheck, Printer } from 'lucide-react';
import { PatientProfile } from '../../types';

interface EmergencyModalProps {
  patient: PatientProfile;
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
}

export const EmergencyModal: React.FC<EmergencyModalProps> = ({ patient, isOpen, onClose }) => {
  if (!isOpen) return null;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-slate-900/60 backdrop-blur-sm animate-in fade-in duration-200">
      <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl max-w-xl w-full shadow-2xl border border-rose-200 overflow-hidden flex flex-col max-h-[90vh]">
        {/* Urgent red header */}

                Fiche d'Urgence Médicale
              </span>

                {patient.full_name}
                <span className="text-xs bg-rose-500/80 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full font-mono">
                  {patient.age} ans • {patient.gender === 'M' ? 'Homme' : 'Femme'}
                </span>
              </h2>
            </div>

        </div>

        {/* Modal content */}

                {patient.blood_type}
              </div>

                <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-rose-700 uppercase">Groupe Sanguin</span>
                <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-rose-950">Rhésus Positif (O+)</p>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 flex items-center gap-3">
              <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-xl bg-teal-600 text-white font-bold flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
                <Heart className="w-6 h-6" />
              </div>
              <div>
                <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase">Immatriculation</span>
                <p className="text-xs font-mono font-semibold text-slate-800">{patient.cin_cnss}</p>
                <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500">{patient.city}, Maroc</p>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Critical Allergies Box */}
          <div className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-amber-50/80 border border-amber-300">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-amber-800 font-bold text-xs uppercase tracking-wide mb-2">
              <AlertTriangle className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-600" />
              <span>Allergies Majeures & Contre-Indications Absolues</span>
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-2">
              {patient.allergies.map((allergy, i) => (
                <div
                  key={i}
                  className="bg-white p-2.5 rounded-lg border border-amber-200 flex items-start justify-between gap-3 shadow-xs"
                >
                  <div>
                    <span className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900">{allergy.name}</span>
                    <p className="text-xs text-rose-600 font-medium">{allergy.reaction}</p>

                    {allergy.severity}

              ))}

          {/* Chronic Conditions & Treatments */}

                >
                  {cond}
                </span>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>

              <a
                href={`tel:${patient.emergency_contact.phone}`}
