              >
                <Phone className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                {patient.emergency_contact.phone}
              </a>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Attending Physician */}
          <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 flex items-center justify-between px-1">
            <span>Médecin référent : <strong className="text-slate-700">{patient.attending_physician}</strong></span>
            <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-teal-600 font-medium">
              <ShieldCheck className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Fiche validée
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Footer actions */}
        <div className="p-4 bg-slate-50 border-t border-slate-100 flex items-center justify-between">
          <button
            onClick={() => window.print()}
            className="px-4 py-2 border border-slate-200 text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-100 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-colors"
          >
            <Printer className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-500" />
            <span>Imprimer la fiche</span>
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={onClose}
            className="px-5 py-2 bg-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-800 text-white rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold transition-colors"
          >
            Fermer
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};


========== FILE: ./src/components/modals/EventFormModal.tsx ==========

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { X, Calendar, Stethoscope, FileText, Activity, AlertCircle, Paperclip } from 'lucide-react';
import { HealthEvent, EventCategory, MedicalDocument } from '../../types';

interface EventFormModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSave: (event: Omit<HealthEvent, 'id' | 'created_at'>, existingId?: string) => void;
  eventToEdit?: HealthEvent | null;
  documents: MedicalDocument[];
}

export const EventFormModal: React.FC<EventFormModalProps> = ({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  onSave,
  eventToEdit,
  documents,
}) => {
  const [title, setTitle] = useState('');
  const [eventDate, setEventDate] = useState(new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10));
  const [category, setCategory] = useState<EventCategory>('CONSULTATION');
  const [doctorOrFacility, setDoctorOrFacility] = useState('');
  const [description, setDescription] = useState('');
  const [treatmentNotes, setTreatmentNotes] = useState('');
  const [bp, setBp] = useState('');
  const [hr, setHr] = useState<string>('');
  const [tempC, setTempC] = useState<string>('');
  const [selectedDocs, setSelectedDocs] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [errors, setErrors] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});

  useEffect(() => {
    if (eventToEdit) {
      setTitle(eventToEdit.title);
      setEventDate(eventToEdit.event_date);
      setCategory(eventToEdit.category);
      setDoctorOrFacility(eventToEdit.doctor_or_facility);
      setDescription(eventToEdit.description);
      setTreatmentNotes(eventToEdit.treatment_notes || '');
      setBp(eventToEdit.vital_signs?.bp || '');
      setHr(eventToEdit.vital_signs?.hr ? String(eventToEdit.vital_signs.hr) : '');
      setTempC(eventToEdit.vital_signs?.temp_c ? String(eventToEdit.vital_signs.temp_c) : '');
      setSelectedDocs(eventToEdit.attachments || []);
    } else {
      setTitle('');
      setEventDate(new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10));
      setCategory('CONSULTATION');
      setDoctorOrFacility('');
      setDescription('');
      setTreatmentNotes('');
      setBp('');
      setHr('');
      setTempC('');
      setSelectedDocs([]);
    }
    setErrors({});
  }, [eventToEdit, isOpen]);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const handleSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const newErrors: Record<string, string> = {};

    if (!title.trim()) newErrors.title = 'Le titre est obligatoire';
    if (!eventDate) newErrors.eventDate = 'La date est obligatoire';
    if (!doctorOrFacility.trim()) newErrors.doctorOrFacility = 'Le médecin ou l\'établissement est obligatoire';
    if (!description.trim()) newErrors.description = 'La description médicale est obligatoire';

    if (Object.keys(newErrors).length > 0) {
      setErrors(newErrors);
      return;
    }

    const vital_signs: HealthEvent['vital_signs'] = {};
    if (bp.trim()) vital_signs.bp = bp.trim();
    if (hr.trim() && !isNaN(Number(hr))) vital_signs.hr = Number(hr);
    if (tempC.trim() && !isNaN(Number(tempC))) vital_signs.temp_c = Number(tempC);

    onSave(
      {
        title: title.trim(),
        event_date: eventDate,
        category,
        doctor_or_facility: doctorOrFacility.trim(),
        description: description.trim(),
        treatment_notes: treatmentNotes.trim() || undefined,
        vital_signs: Object.keys(vital_signs).length > 0 ? vital_signs : undefined,
        attachments: selectedDocs,
      },
      eventToEdit ? eventToEdit.id : undefined
    );
    onClose();
  };

  const toggleDocument = (docId: string) => {
    setSelectedDocs((prev) =>
      prev.includes(docId) ? prev.filter((id) => id !== docId) : [...prev, docId]
    );
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-slate-900/60 backdrop-blur-sm animate-in fade-in duration-200">
      <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl max-w-xl w-full shadow-2xl border border-slate-200 overflow-hidden flex flex-col max-h-[90vh]">
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-teal-700 to-teal-800 px-6 py-4 text-white flex items-center justify-between">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-white/20 backdrop-blur-md flex items-center justify-center">
              <Stethoscope className="w-5 h-5 text-white" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <span className="text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider font-bold text-teal-200">
                Carnet de Santé Numérique
              </span>
              <h2 className="text-base font-bold text-white">
                {eventToEdit ? 'Modifier l\'événement médical' : 'Enregistrer un nouvel événement de santé'}
              </h2>
            </div>
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={onClose}
            className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-white/10 hover:bg-white/20 flex items-center justify-center transition-colors text-white"
          >
            <X className="w-5 h-5" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Form Body */}
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="p-6 overflow-y-auto space-y-4 flex-1">
          {/* Category & Date */}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                Catégorie médicale <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
              </label>
              <select
                value={category}
                onChange={(e) => setCategory(e.target.value as EventCategory)}
                className="w-full text-xs rounded-xl border border-slate-300 p-2.5 focus:border-teal-500 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-teal-500 bg-white"
              >
                <option value="CONSULTATION">🩺 Consultation Médicale</option>
                <option value="ANALYSE">🧪 Analyse & Biologie</option>
                <option value="TRAITEMENT">💊 Traitement & Prescription</option>
                <option value="VACCIN">💉 Vaccination</option>
                <option value="CHIRURGIE">🏥 Intervention / Chirurgie</option>
                <option value="SYMPTOME">⚠️ Symptôme / Observation</option>
              </select>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                Date de l'événement <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
              </label>
              <div className="relative">
                <input
                  type="date"
                  value={eventDate}
                  onChange={(e) => setEventDate(e.target.value)}
                  className={`w-full text-xs rounded-xl border ${
                    errors.eventDate ? 'border-rose-400' : 'border-slate-300'
                  } p-2.5 focus:border-teal-500 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-teal-500`}
                />
              </div>
              {errors.eventDate && (
                <span className="text-[11px] text-rose-500 flex items-center gap-1 mt-1">
                  <AlertCircle className="w-3 h-3" /> {errors.eventDate}
                </span>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Title */}
          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
              Titre de l'événement <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
            </label>
            <input
              type="text"
              placeholder="Ex: Consultation cardiologie, Bilan sanguin à jeun, Rappel DTP..."
              value={title}
              onChange={(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)}
              className={`w-full text-xs rounded-xl border ${
                errors.title ? 'border-rose-400' : 'border-slate-300'
              } p-2.5 focus:border-teal-500 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-teal-500`}
            />
            {errors.title && (
              <span className="text-[11px] text-rose-500 flex items-center gap-1 mt-1">
                <AlertCircle className="w-3 h-3" /> {errors.title}
              </span>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Doctor or Facility */}
          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
              Praticien ou Établissement de santé <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
            </label>
            <input
              type="text"
              placeholder="Ex: Dr. Omar Bennani (Clinique Agdal, Rabat), Hôpital Cheikh Khalifa..."
              value={doctorOrFacility}
              onChange={(e) => setDoctorOrFacility(e.target.value)}
              className={`w-full text-xs rounded-xl border ${
                errors.doctorOrFacility ? 'border-rose-400' : 'border-slate-300'
              } p-2.5 focus:border-teal-500 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-teal-500`}
            />
            {errors.doctorOrFacility && (
              <span className="text-[11px] text-rose-500 flex items-center gap-1 mt-1">
                <AlertCircle className="w-3 h-3" /> {errors.doctorOrFacility}
              </span>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Description */}
          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
              Description & Constatations cliniques <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
            </label>
            <textarea
              rows={3}
              placeholder="Détails du motif, compte-rendu médical, conclusions de l'examen..."
              value={description}
              onChange={(e) => setDescription(e.target.value)}
              className={`w-full text-xs rounded-xl border ${
                errors.description ? 'border-rose-400' : 'border-slate-300'
              } p-2.5 focus:border-teal-500 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-teal-500`}
            />
            {errors.description && (
              <span className="text-[11px] text-rose-500 flex items-center gap-1 mt-1">
                <AlertCircle className="w-3 h-3" /> {errors.description}
              </span>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Treatment Notes */}
          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
              Prescription ou Recommandations de traitement (Optionnel)
            </label>
            <input
              type="text"
              placeholder="Ex: Ventoline 100µg en cas de crise, prochain bilan dans 6 mois..."
              value={treatmentNotes}
              onChange={(e) => setTreatmentNotes(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full text-xs rounded-xl border border-slate-300 p-2.5 focus:border-teal-500 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-teal-500"
            />
          </div>

          {/* Vitals */}
          <div className="p-3 bg-slate-50 rounded-xl border border-slate-200">
            <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-600 uppercase tracking-wider flex items-center gap-1.5 mb-2">
              <Activity className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-teal-600" />
              Constantes vitales relevées (Optionnel)
            </span>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-[10px] text-slate-500 mb-0.5">Tension (mmHg)</label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  placeholder="120/80"
                  value={bp}
                  onChange={(e) => setBp(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full text-xs rounded-lg border border-slate-200 p-1.5 bg-white text-center"
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="block text-[10px] text-slate-500 mb-0.5">Pouls (bpm)</label>
                <input
                  type="number"
                  placeholder="72"
                  value={hr}
                  onChange={(e) => setHr(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full text-xs rounded-lg border border-slate-200 p-1.5 bg-white text-center"
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="block text-[10px] text-slate-500 mb-0.5">Température (°C)</label>
                <input
                  type="number"
                  step="0.1"
                  placeholder="37.0"
                  value={tempC}
                  onChange={(e) => setTempC(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full text-xs rounded-lg border border-slate-200 p-1.5 bg-white text-center"
                />
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Link documents from vault */}
          {documents.length > 0 && (
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <Paperclip className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-500" />
                Associer des documents du Coffre-fort ({selectedDocs.length} sélectionné{selectedDocs.length > 1 ? 's' : ''})
              </label>
              <div className="max-h-32 overflow-y-auto space-y-1.5 p-2 bg-slate-50 rounded-xl border border-slate-200">
                {documents.map((doc) => {
                  const isChecked = selectedDocs.includes(doc.id);
                  return (
                    <label
                      key={doc.id}
                      className={`flex items-center gap-2 p-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs cursor-pointer transition-colors ${
                        isChecked ? 'bg-teal-50 border border-teal-200 font-semibold text-teal-900' : 'hover:bg-white text-slate-700'
                      }`}
                    >
                      <input
                        type="checkbox"
                        checked={isChecked}
                        onChange={() => toggleDocument(doc.id)}
                        className="rounded text-teal-600 focus:ring-teal-500"
                      />
                      <FileText className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400 shrink-0" />
                      <span className="truncate flex-1">{doc.title}</span>
                      <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-mono">{doc.issue_date}</span>
                    </label>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Submit */}
          <div className="pt-2 flex items-center justify-end gap-2">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onClose}
              className="px-4 py-2 border border-slate-200 text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold transition-colors"
            >
              Annuler
            </button>
            <button
              type="submit"
              className="px-5 py-2 bg-teal-600 hover:bg-teal-700 text-white rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition-colors shadow-xs"
            >
              {eventToEdit ? 'Enregistrer les modifications' : 'Ajouter au carnet'}
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};


========== FILE: ./src/components/views/AuditLogView.tsx ==========

import React, { useState, useMemo } from 'react';
import {
  History,
  ShieldCheck,
  ShieldAlert,
  Search,
  Filter,
  User,
  Stethoscope,
  Globe,
  Clock,
  Printer,
  Download,
  AlertTriangle,
  Lock,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { AuditLogEntry, AuditActionType } from '../../types';

interface AuditLogViewProps {
  logs: AuditLogEntry[];
}

export const AuditLogView: React.FC<AuditLogViewProps> = ({ logs }) => {
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [selectedFilter, setSelectedFilter] = useState<string>('ALL');

  const filteredLogs = useMemo(() => {
    return logs
      .filter((log) => {
        const matchesFilter =
          selectedFilter === 'ALL' ||
          (selectedFilter === 'DOCTOR' && log.actor_role === 'MEDECIN') ||
          (selectedFilter === 'PATIENT' && log.actor_role === 'PATIENT') ||
          (selectedFilter === 'SHARING' && log.action.startsWith('SESSION_'));

        const matchesQuery =
          searchQuery.trim() === '' ||
          log.actor.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) ||
          log.details.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) ||
          log.ip_address.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase());

        return matchesFilter && matchesQuery;
      })
      .sort((a, b) => new Date(b.timestamp).getTime() - new Date(a.timestamp).getTime());
  }, [logs, selectedFilter, searchQuery]);

  const getActionBadge = (action: AuditActionType, status: AuditLogEntry['status']) => {
    if (status === 'REVOKED') {
      return {
        label: 'Révocation immédiate',
        color: 'bg-rose-50 text-rose-700 border-rose-200',
        icon: ShieldAlert,
      };
    }
    switch (action) {
      case 'SESSION_ACCESSED':
        return {
          label: 'Consultation Médecin',
          color: 'bg-teal-50 text-teal-700 border-teal-200',
          icon: Stethoscope,
        };
      case 'SESSION_CREATED':
        return {
          label: 'Génération QR Code',
          color: 'bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 border-blue-200',
          icon: ShieldCheck,
        };
      case 'SESSION_REVOKED':
        return {
          label: 'Révocation',
          color: 'bg-rose-50 text-rose-700 border-rose-200',
          icon: ShieldAlert,
        };
      case 'DOCUMENT_UPLOADED':
        return {
          label: 'Dépôt Coffre-fort',
          color: 'bg-purple-50 text-purple-700 border-purple-200',
          icon: Lock,
        };
      case 'RECORD_CREATED':
      case 'RECORD_UPDATED':
        return {
          label: 'Mise à jour Carnet',
          color: 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 border-slate-200',
          icon: History,
        };
      default:
        return {
          label: action,
          color: 'bg-slate-50 text-slate-600 border-slate-200',
          icon: History,
        };
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-start sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <h1 className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 tracking-tight">
              Journal d'Audit & Traçabilité Souveraine
            </h1>
            <span className="text-[10px] font-bold bg-teal-50 text-teal-700 border border-teal-200 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full flex items-center gap-1">
              <ShieldCheck className="w-3 h-3" /> Immuable
            </span>
          </div>
          <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-500 mt-0.5">
            Historique certifié et horodaté de toutes les créations, accès médecins et révocations
          </p>
        </div>

        <button
          onClick={() => window.print()}
          className="px-4 py-2 border border-slate-200 bg-white hover:bg-slate-50 text-slate-700 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-2 shadow-2xs transition-colors"
        >
          <Printer className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-500" />
          <span>Exporter le rapport d'audit</span>
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Filter and Search Bar */}
      <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200/80 shadow-xs space-y-3">
        <div className="relative">
          <Search className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2" />
          <input
            type="text"
            placeholder="Rechercher par acteur, adresse IP, détail de consultation..."
            value={searchQuery}
            onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
            className="w-full text-xs rounded-xl border border-slate-200 pl-10 pr-4 py-2.5 bg-slate-50/50 focus:bg-white focus:border-teal-500 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-teal-500 transition-all"
          />
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          {[
            { id: 'ALL', label: 'Toutes les activités' },
            { id: 'DOCTOR', label: 'Accès Médecins' },
            { id: 'SHARING', label: 'Partages & Sessions QR' },
            { id: 'PATIENT', label: 'Actions Patient' },
          ].map((f) => (
            <button
              key={f.id}
              onClick={() => setSelectedFilter(f.id)}
              className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold transition-all ${
                selectedFilter === f.id
                  ? 'bg-slate-900 text-white shadow-2xs'
                  : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-200/70 hover:text-slate-900'
              }`}
            >
              {f.label}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Audit Log Table / List */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200/80 shadow-xs overflow-hidden">
        {filteredLogs.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="p-12 text-center">
            <History className="w-10 h-10 text-slate-300 mx-auto mb-2" />
            <h3 className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-700">Aucun enregistrement d'audit</h3>
            <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 mt-0.5">
              Aucune activité ne correspond à vos filtres.
            </p>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="divide-y divide-slate-100">
            {filteredLogs.map((log) => {
              const badge = getActionBadge(log.action, log.status);
              const BadgeIcon = badge.icon;
              return (
                <div
                  key={log.id}
                  className="p-4 sm:p-5 hover:bg-slate-50/60 transition-colors flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-start sm:items-center justify-between gap-4"
                >
                  <div className="flex items-start gap-3.5 min-w-0">
                    <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 mt-0.5">
                      <BadgeIcon className="w-5 h-5 text-slate-600" />
                    </div>

                    <div className="min-w-0">
                      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 mb-1">
                        <span className={`text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md border ${badge.color}`}>
                          {badge.label}
                        </span>
                        <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-900">
                          {log.actor}
                        </span>
                        <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-mono flex items-center gap-1">
                          <Globe className="w-3 h-3" />
                          {log.ip_address}
                        </span>
                      </div>

                      <p className="text-xs text-slate-600 leading-relaxed">
                        {log.details}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="text-left sm:text-right shrink-0 self-end sm:self-center font-mono">
                    <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-semibold block">
                      {new Date(log.timestamp).toLocaleDateString('fr-FR', {
                        day: '2-digit',
                        month: 'short',
                        year: 'numeric',
                      })}
                    </span>
                    <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 flex items-center gap-1 sm:justify-end">
                      <Clock className="w-3 h-3" />
                      {new Date(log.timestamp).toLocaleTimeString('fr-FR', {
                        hour: '2-digit',
                        minute: '2-digit',
                        second: '2-digit',
                      })}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Security explanation note */}
      <div className="p-4 bg-teal-50/70 border border-teal-200 rounded-2xl flex items-start gap-3 text-xs text-teal-900">
        <ShieldCheck className="w-5 h-5 text-teal-600 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
        <p className="leading-relaxed">
          <strong>Principe de non-répudiation :</strong> Chaque scan de QR code, téléchargement de document ou tentative d'accès est tracé de manière indélébile dans ce journal d'audit afin de garantir au patient la transparence absolue sur l'usage de ses données de santé.
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};


========== FILE: ./src/components/views/DashboardView.tsx ==========

import React from 'react';
import {
  Activity,
  Heart,
  Droplet,
  Scale,
  PlusCircle,
  UploadCloud,
  QrCode,
  AlertTriangle,
  ArrowRight,
  ShieldCheck,
  Calendar,
  Clock,
  ExternalLink,
  FolderLock,
  Stethoscope,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { PatientProfile, HealthEvent, MedicalDocument, SharingSession } from '../../types';
import { TabType } from '../layout/Sidebar';

interface DashboardViewProps {
  patient: PatientProfile;
  events: HealthEvent[];
  documents: MedicalDocument[];
  sharingSessions: SharingSession[];
  onNavigate: (tab: TabType) => void;
  onOpenNewEvent: () => void;
  onOpenUploadDoc: () => void;
  onOpenEmergency: () => void;
  onOpenDoctorPortal: (token?: string) => void;
  onRevokeSession: (sessionId: string) => void;
}

export const DashboardView: React.FC<DashboardViewProps> = ({
  patient,
  events,
  documents,
  sharingSessions,
  onNavigate,
  onOpenNewEvent,
  onOpenUploadDoc,
  onOpenEmergency,
  onOpenDoctorPortal,
  onRevokeSession,
}) => {
  const activeSessions = sharingSessions.filter(
    (s) => !s.is_revoked && new Date(s.expires_at).getTime() > Date.now()
  );

  const recentEvents = [...events]
    .sort((a, b) => new Date(b.event_date).getTime() - new Date(a.event_date).getTime())
    .slice(0, 4);

  const getCategoryBadge = (cat: HealthEvent['category']) => {
    switch (cat) {
      case 'CONSULTATION':
        return { label: 'Consultation', color: 'bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 border-blue-200' };
      case 'ANALYSE':
        return { label: 'Biologie', color: 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border-emerald-200' };
      case 'TRAITEMENT':
        return { label: 'Traitement', color: 'bg-purple-50 text-purple-700 border-purple-200' };
      case 'VACCIN':
        return { label: 'Vaccination', color: 'bg-teal-50 text-teal-700 border-teal-200' };
      case 'CHIRURGIE':
        return { label: 'Intervention', color: 'bg-rose-50 text-rose-700 border-rose-200' };
      case 'SYMPTOME':
        return { label: 'Symptôme', color: 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border-amber-200' };
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* Patient Hero Banner */}
      <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-slate-900 via-slate-800 to-teal-950 rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-8 text-white shadow-xl relative overflow-hidden">
        {/* Decorative backdrop shapes */}
        <div className="absolute top-0 right-0 w-80 h-80 bg-teal-500/10 rounded-full blur-3xl pointer-events-none" />
        <div className="absolute -bottom-10 -left-10 w-60 h-60 bg-teal-600/10 rounded-full blur-2xl pointer-events-none" />

        <div className="relative z-10 flex flex-col md:flex-row items-start md:items-center justify-between gap-6">
          <div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2">
              <span className="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-teal-300">
                Dossier Médical Souverain
              </span>
              <span className="text-slate-400">•</span>
              <span className="text-xs text-slate-300 font-mono">{patient.cin_cnss}</span>
            </div>
            <h1 className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-extrabold text-white tracking-tight">
              Bonjour, {patient.full_name}
            </h1>
            <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-300 mt-1 max-w-xl">
              Votre carnet de santé numérique est à jour. Vous contrôlez à 100% l'accès à vos données médicales et vos partages éphémères.
            </p>

            {/* Critical Allergies Badge */}
            {patient.allergies.length > 0 && (
              <div className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                <span className="text-xs text-rose-300 font-semibold flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <AlertTriangle className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-rose-400" />
                  Allergie majeure déclarée :
                </span>
                {patient.allergies.map((all, i) => (
                  <span
                    key={i}
                    className="px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-rose-500/20 text-rose-200 border border-rose-500/30 text-xs font-bold"
                  >
                    {all.name} ({all.severity})
                  </span>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Quick Action Buttons */}
          <div className="flex flex-wrap sm:flex-col gap-2 shrink-0 w-full sm:w-auto">
            <button
              onClick={onOpenNewEvent}
              className="flex-1 sm:flex-none px-4 py-2.5 bg-teal-600 hover:bg-teal-500 text-white rounded-xl text-xs font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-md transition-all hover:scale-[1.02]"
            >
              <PlusCircle className="w-4 h-4" />
              <span>Nouvel événement</span>
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => onNavigate('sharing')}
              className="flex-1 sm:flex-none px-4 py-2.5 bg-white/10 hover:bg-white/20 text-white border border-white/20 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-2 backdrop-blur-md transition-all"

              <QrCode className="w-4 h-4 text-teal-300" />
              <span>Partage QR Médecin</span>

          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Vitals Summary Cards */}

        {/* Tension */}
        <div className="bg-white p-4 sm:p-5 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200/80 shadow-xs hover:shadow-md transition-all">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-slate-400 mb-2">
            <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500">Tension Artérielle</span>
            <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-xl bg-teal-50 text-teal-600 flex items-center justify-center">

            <ShieldCheck className="w-3 h-3" /> Pression optimale
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Fréquence Cardiaque */}
        <div className="bg-white p-4 sm:p-5 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200/80 shadow-xs hover:shadow-md transition-all">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-slate-400 mb-2">
            <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500">Pouls au repos</span>

              <Heart className="w-4 h-4" />

              onClick={() => onNavigate('journal')}
