            >
              <span>Voir tout le carnet</span>
              <ArrowRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5 group-hover:translate-x-0.5 transition-transform" />
            </button>
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-3.5">
            {recentEvents.map((event) => {
              const badge = getCategoryBadge(event.category);
              return (
                <div
                  key={event.id}
                  onClick={() => onNavigate('journal')}
                  className="p-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-100 hover:border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50/70 transition-all cursor-pointer flex items-start justify-between gap-4"
                >
                  <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1">
                      <span className={`text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md border ${badge.color}`}>
                        {badge.label}
                      </span>
                      <span className="text-xs text-slate-400 font-mono flex items-center gap-1">
                        <Calendar className="w-3 h-3" />
                        {event.event_date}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 truncate">{event.title}</h3>
                    <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5 line-clamp-1">{event.doctor_or_facility}</p>
                    <p className="text-xs text-slate-600 mt-1 line-clamp-2">{event.description}</p>
                  </div>

                  {event.vital_signs?.bp && (
                    <div className="hidden sm:block text-right shrink-0">
                      <span className="text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-slate-400 block">Tension</span>
                      <span className="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-teal-800">{event.vital_signs.bp}</span>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Vault Quick Access Card */}
        <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200/80 p-6 shadow-xs flex flex-col justify-between">
          <div>
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 text-white flex items-center justify-center">
                  <FolderLock className="w-4 h-4" />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900">Coffre-fort Médical</h3>
                  <span className="text-xs text-slate-500">{documents.length} documents certifiés</span>
                </div>
              </div>
              <button
                onClick={onOpenUploadDoc}
                className="p-1.5 rounded-lg bg-teal-50 text-teal-700 hover:bg-teal-100 text-xs font-semibold"
                title="Ajouter un document"
              >
                <PlusCircle className="w-4 h-4" />
              </button>
            </div>

            <p className="text-xs text-slate-600 mb-4 leading-relaxed">
              Vos ordonnances, bilans biologiques et comptes-rendus numérisés avec prévisualisation certifiée.
            </p>

            {/* List of 3 recent documents */}
            <div className="space-y-2">
              {documents.slice(0, 3).map((doc) => (
                <div
                  key={doc.id}
                  onClick={() => onNavigate('vault')}
                  className="p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 hover:bg-slate-100 transition-colors cursor-pointer flex items-center justify-between gap-2 border border-slate-100"
                >
                  <div className="min-w-0">
                    <p className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-800 truncate">{doc.title}</p>
                    <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-mono">
                      {doc.doctor_or_lab} • {doc.issue_date}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-teal-700 bg-teal-50 px-2 py-0.5 rounded">
                    {doc.category}
                  </span>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="pt-5 border-t border-slate-100 mt-4">
            <button
              onClick={() => onNavigate('vault')}
              className="w-full py-2.5 bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-800 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 transition-colors"
            >
              <span>Accéder au Coffre-fort</span>
              <ArrowRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};


========== FILE: ./src/components/views/DoctorPortalView.tsx ==========

import React, { useMemo } from 'react';
import {
  Stethoscope,
  ShieldCheck,
  ShieldAlert,
  AlertTriangle,
  Clock,
  Heart,
  Activity,
  Calendar,
  FileText,
  User,
  Phone,
  ArrowLeft,
  Building,
  CheckCircle2,
  Lock,
} from 'lucide-react';
import {
  PatientProfile,
  HealthEvent,
  MedicalDocument,
  SharingSession,
} from '../../types';

interface DoctorPortalViewProps {
  token?: string;
  patient: PatientProfile;
  events: HealthEvent[];
  documents: MedicalDocument[];
  sessions: SharingSession[];
  onBackToApp: () => void;
  onPreviewDocument: (doc: MedicalDocument) => void;
}

export const DoctorPortalView: React.FC<DoctorPortalViewProps> = ({
  token,
  patient,
  events,
  documents,
  sessions,
  onBackToApp,
  onPreviewDocument,
}) => {
  // Find session by token or pick first active session
  const session = useMemo(() => {
    if (token) {
      return sessions.find((s) => s.token === token);
    }
    return sessions.find((s) => !s.is_revoked) || sessions[0];
  }, [sessions, token]);

  const isExpired = session ? new Date(session.expires_at).getTime() < Date.now() : false;
  const isRevoked = session ? session.is_revoked : false;
  const isInvalid = !session || isRevoked || isExpired;

  // Filter records by patient's explicit consent scope
  const consentedEvents = useMemo(() => {
    if (!session) return [];
    if (session.scope.all_events) return events;
    return events.filter((e) => session.scope.event_ids.includes(e.id));
  }, [events, session]);

  const consentedDocuments = useMemo(() => {
    if (!session) return [];
    if (session.scope.all_docs) return documents;
    return documents.filter((d) => session.scope.doc_ids.includes(d.id));
  }, [documents, session]);

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-100 flex flex-col">
      {/* Top Professional Portal Header */}
      <header className="bg-slate-900 text-white px-6 py-4 shadow-md sticky top-0 z-20">
        <div className="max-w-5xl mx-auto flex items-center justify-between">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-teal-500/20 text-teal-400 border border-teal-500/30 flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-lg">
              <Stethoscope className="w-5 h-5" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <span className="font-extrabold text-base tracking-tight text-white">
                  CARNI • Portail Professionnel de Santé
                </span>
                <span className="text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-teal-500/20 text-teal-300 border border-teal-500/30">
                  MAROC SANTE
                </span>
              </div>
              <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">
                Consultation médicale temporaire sous contrôle exclusif du patient
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <button
            onClick={onBackToApp}
            className="px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-300 hover:text-white text-xs font-semibold flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-colors"
          >
            <ArrowLeft className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            <span>Quitter la session</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </header>

      {/* Main Body */}
      <main className="max-w-5xl w-full mx-auto p-4 sm:p-6 flex-1 space-y-6">
        {isInvalid ? (
          /* Access Denied / Expired Screen */
          <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl border border-rose-200 p-10 text-center shadow-lg max-w-xl mx-auto my-12">
            <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-2xl bg-rose-50 text-rose-600 border border-rose-200 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-4">
              <ShieldAlert className="w-8 h-8" />
            </div>
            <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900">
              Accès Médical Expiré ou Révoqué
            </h2>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-600 mt-2 leading-relaxed">
              Le jeton de consultation éphémère transmis via QR code n'est plus valide. Le patient a révoqué l'accès ou la durée maximale accordée est écoulée.
            </p>
            <div className="mt-4 p-3 bg-slate-50 rounded-xl text-left font-mono text-[11px] text-slate-500 border border-slate-200 space-y-1">
              <div>• Jeton : {token || 'N/A'}</div>
              <div>• Statut : {isRevoked ? 'Révoqué par le patient' : isExpired ? 'Délai d\'expiration dépassé' : 'Jeton invalide'}</div>
              <div>• Sécurité : Données médicales verrouillées</div>
            </div>
            <button
              onClick={onBackToApp}
              className="mt-6 px-6 py-2.5 bg-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-800 text-white rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition-colors"
            >
              Retour à l'accueil
            </button>
          </div>
        ) : (
          /* Valid Consented Doctor View */
          <>
            {/* Live Security Bar */}
            <div className="bg-teal-50 border border-teal-200 rounded-2xl p-4 flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-start sm:items-center justify-between gap-3 shadow-xs">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-teal-600 text-white flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
                  <ShieldCheck className="w-5 h-5" />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <span className="text-xs font-bold text-teal-900">
                      Accès Autorisé pour : {session.recipient_label}
                    </span>
                    <span className="text-[10px] bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full font-bold">
                      Session Active
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-teal-800 mt-0.5">
                    Expire le {new Date(session.expires_at).toLocaleTimeString('fr-FR')} • Traçabilité CNIL/CNDP active
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-mono self-end sm:self-auto">
                ID Session: {session.token.slice(0, 12)}...
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Patient Header Banner */}
            <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 p-6 shadow-xs">
              <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-start sm:items-center justify-between gap-4 pb-4 border-b border-slate-100">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3.5">
                  <div className="w-14 h-14 rounded-2xl bg-slate-900 text-white flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-lg shadow-sm">
                    {patient.full_name.split(' ').map((n) => n[0]).join('')}
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-2">
                      {patient.full_name}
                      <span className="text-xs font-normal text-slate-500">
                        ({patient.age} ans, {patient.gender === 'M' ? 'Homme' : 'Femme'})
                      </span>
                    </h2>
                    <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-mono mt-0.5">
                      Identifiant : {patient.cin_cnss} • {patient.city}, Maroc
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                  <div className="text-right">
                    <span className="text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-slate-400 block">Groupe Sanguin</span>
                    <span className="text-sm font-black text-rose-600 bg-rose-50 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg border border-rose-200">
                      {patient.blood_type}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Patient Alerts & Vitals (if consented) */}
              <div className="mt-4 grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                {/* Allergies Alert */}
                {session.scope.include_allergies && patient.allergies.length > 0 && (
                  <div className="p-3.5 bg-rose-50/80 border border-rose-200 rounded-xl">
                    <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-rose-800 uppercase flex items-center gap-1.5 mb-2">
                      <AlertTriangle className="w-4 h-4 text-rose-600" />
                      Allergies & Alertes Vitales
                    </span>
                    <div className="space-y-1.5">
                      {patient.allergies.map((all, i) => (
                        <div key={i} className="text-xs text-rose-950 font-semibold bg-white p-2 rounded-lg border border-rose-100">
                          <strong>{all.name} :</strong> {all.reaction} ({all.severity})
                        </div>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}

                {/* Vitals */}
                {session.scope.include_vitals && (
                  <div className="p-3.5 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl">
                    <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-600 uppercase flex items-center gap-1.5 mb-2">
                      <Activity className="w-4 h-4 text-teal-600" />
                      Dernières Constantes
                    </span>
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 text-center">
                      <div className="bg-white p-2 rounded-lg border border-slate-100">
                        <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 block">Tension</span>
                        <span className="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-slate-900">{patient.vitals.blood_pressure}</span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="bg-white p-2 rounded-lg border border-slate-100">
                        <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 block">Pouls</span>
                        <span className="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-slate-900">{patient.vitals.heart_rate} bpm</span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="bg-white p-2 rounded-lg border border-slate-100">
                        <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 block">Glycémie</span>
                        <span className="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-slate-900">{patient.vitals.blood_sugar_mg_dl} mg/dL</span>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>

              {/* Emergency Contact */}
              {session.scope.include_emergency && (
                <div className="mt-4 pt-3 border-t border-slate-100 text-xs text-slate-600 flex items-center justify-between">
                  <span>
                    Contact d'urgence : <strong>{patient.emergency_contact.name} ({patient.emergency_contact.relation})</strong>
                  </span>
                  <a
                    href={`tel:${patient.emergency_contact.phone}`}
                    className="font-bold text-teal-700 hover:underline flex items-center gap-1"
                  >
                    <Phone className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                    {patient.emergency_contact.phone}
                  </a>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Consented Health Events */}
            <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 p-6 shadow-xs">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
                <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 uppercase tracking-wide flex items-center gap-2">
                  <Calendar className="w-4 h-4 text-teal-600" />
                  Antécédents & Événements Médicaux Transmis ({consentedEvents.length})
                </h3>
              </div>

              {consentedEvents.length === 0 ? (
                <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 italic">
                  Aucun événement du carnet n'a été sélectionné pour ce partage.
                </p>
              ) : (
                <div className="space-y-3">
                  {consentedEvents.map((evt) => (
                    <div
                      key={evt.id}
                      className="p-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-100 bg-slate-50/50 space-y-1.5"
                    >
                      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                        <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-900">{evt.title}</span>
                        <span className="text-xs font-mono text-slate-400">{evt.event_date}</span>
                      </div>
                      <p className="text-xs text-teal-800 font-semibold">{evt.doctor_or_facility}</p>
                      <p className="text-xs text-slate-600 leading-relaxed">{evt.description}</p>
                      {evt.treatment_notes && (
                        <p className="text-xs text-purple-900 font-medium bg-purple-50 p-2 rounded-lg mt-2">
                          Prescription / Notes : {evt.treatment_notes}
                        </p>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Consented Medical Documents */}
            <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 p-6 shadow-xs">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
                <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 uppercase tracking-wide flex items-center gap-2">
                  <FileText className="w-4 h-4 text-teal-600" />
                  Documents Médicaux Transmis ({consentedDocuments.length})
                </h3>
              </div>

              {consentedDocuments.length === 0 ? (
                <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 italic">
                  Aucun document numérisé n'a été sélectionné pour ce partage.
                </p>
              ) : (
                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                  {consentedDocuments.map((doc) => (
                    <div
                      key={doc.id}
                      className="p-3.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 hover:border-teal-300 bg-slate-50/40 flex items-start justify-between gap-3"
                    >
                      <div className="min-w-0">
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1">
                          <span className="text-[10px] font-bold px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-teal-50 text-teal-800 border border-teal-200">
                            {doc.category}
                          </span>
                          <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-mono">{doc.issue_date}</span>
                        </div>
                        <h4 className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-900 truncate">{doc.title}</h4>
                        <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 truncate">{doc.doctor_or_lab}</p>
                      </div>

                      <button
                        onClick={() => onPreviewDocument(doc)}
                        className="px-2.5 py-1.5 bg-teal-600 hover:bg-teal-700 text-white rounded-lg text-xs font-bold shrink-0 transition-colors"
                      >
                        Aperçu
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Medical Audit Notice */}
            <div className="p-4 bg-slate-200/50 rounded-2xl text-center text-xs text-slate-500">
              <span className="block font-medium">
                Accès temporaire audité • Toute consultation est enregistrée dans le journal de traçabilité du patient.
              </span>
            </div>
          </>
        )}
      </main>
    </div>
  );
};


========== FILE: ./src/components/views/JournalView.tsx ==========

import React, { useState, useMemo } from 'react';
import {
  Plus,
  Search,
  Filter,
  Calendar,
  Stethoscope,
  Pill,
  Syringe,
  AlertTriangle,
  FileText,
  Activity,
  Edit2,
  Trash2,
  Paperclip,
  CheckCircle2,
  Heart,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { HealthEvent, EventCategory, MedicalDocument } from '../../types';

interface JournalViewProps {
  events: HealthEvent[];
  documents: MedicalDocument[];
  onOpenNewEvent: () => void;
  onEditEvent: (event: HealthEvent) => void;
  onDeleteEvent: (id: string) => void;
  onPreviewDocument: (doc: MedicalDocument) => void;
}

export const JournalView: React.FC<JournalViewProps> = ({
  events,
  documents,
  onOpenNewEvent,
  onEditEvent,
  onDeleteEvent,
  onPreviewDocument,
}) => {
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [selectedCategory, setSelectedCategory] = useState<string>('ALL');
  const [selectedEventForDetail, setSelectedEventForDetail] = useState<HealthEvent | null>(null);

  const categories: { id: string; label: string; icon: any }[] = [
    { id: 'ALL', label: 'Tous', icon: Filter },
    { id: 'CONSULTATION', label: 'Consultations', icon: Stethoscope },
    { id: 'ANALYSE', label: 'Analyses & Biologie', icon: Activity },
    { id: 'TRAITEMENT', label: 'Traitements', icon: Pill },
    { id: 'VACCIN', label: 'Vaccinations', icon: Syringe },
    { id: 'CHIRURGIE', label: 'Chirurgies', icon: Heart },
    { id: 'SYMPTOME', label: 'Symptômes', icon: AlertTriangle },
  ];

  const filteredEvents = useMemo(() => {
    return events
      .filter((evt) => {
        const matchesCategory =
          selectedCategory === 'ALL' || evt.category === selectedCategory;
        const matchesQuery =
          searchQuery.trim() === '' ||
          evt.title.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) ||
          evt.doctor_or_facility.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) ||
          evt.description.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase());
        return matchesCategory && matchesQuery;
      })
      .sort((a, b) => new Date(b.event_date).getTime() - new Date(a.event_date).getTime());
  }, [events, selectedCategory, searchQuery]);

  const getCategoryConfig = (cat: EventCategory) => {
    switch (cat) {
      case 'CONSULTATION':
        return { label: 'Consultation', badge: 'bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 border-blue-200' };
      case 'ANALYSE':
        return { label: 'Analyse / Biologie', badge: 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border-emerald-200' };
      case 'TRAITEMENT':
        return { label: 'Traitement', badge: 'bg-purple-50 text-purple-700 border-purple-200' };
      case 'VACCIN':
        return { label: 'Vaccin', badge: 'bg-teal-50 text-teal-700 border-teal-200' };
      case 'CHIRURGIE':
        return { label: 'Chirurgie', badge: 'bg-rose-50 text-rose-700 border-rose-200' };
      case 'SYMPTOME':
        return { label: 'Symptôme', badge: 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border-amber-200' };
    }
  };

  const docMap = useMemo(() => {
    const map = new Map<string, MedicalDocument>();
    documents.forEach((d) => map.set(d.id, d));
    return map;
  }, [documents]);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* Top Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-start sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 tracking-tight">
            Carnet de Santé Numérique
          </h1>
          <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-500 mt-0.5">
            Historique chronologique complet de vos consultations, examens et traitements
          </p>
        </div>

        <button
          onClick={onOpenNewEvent}
          className="px-4 py-2.5 bg-teal-600 hover:bg-teal-700 text-white rounded-xl text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-2 shadow-sm transition-all hover:scale-[1.02]"
        >
          <Plus className="w-4 h-4" />
          <span>Ajouter un événement</span>
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Filter and Search Bar */}
      <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200/80 shadow-xs space-y-3">
        <div className="relative">
          <Search className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2" />
          <input
            type="text"
            placeholder="Rechercher par titre, médecin, clinique, mot-clé clinique..."
            value={searchQuery}
            onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
            className="w-full text-xs rounded-xl border border-slate-200 pl-10 pr-4 py-2.5 bg-slate-50/50 focus:bg-white focus:border-teal-500 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-teal-500 transition-all"
          />
        </div>

        {/* Category Pill Filters */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 overflow-x-auto pb-1 scrollbar-none">
          {categories.map((cat) => {
            const isSelected = selectedCategory === cat.id;
            return (
              <button
                key={cat.id}
                onClick={() => setSelectedCategory(cat.id)}
                className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold whitespace-nowrap transition-all flex items-center gap-1.5 ${
                  isSelected
                    ? 'bg-slate-900 text-white shadow-2xs'
                    : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-200/70 hover:text-slate-900'
                }`}
              >
                <span>{cat.label}</span>
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Events Timeline / Cards */}
      {filteredEvents.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200/80 p-12 text-center">
          <FileText className="w-10 h-10 text-slate-300 mx-auto mb-3" />
          <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-700">Aucun événement trouvé</h3>
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-1 max-w-sm mx-auto">
            Aucun enregistrement ne correspond à vos critères de recherche. Essayez de réinitialiser les filtres.
          </p>
          <button
            onClick={() => {
              setSearchQuery('');
              setSelectedCategory('ALL');
            }}
            className="mt-4 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs text-teal-600 font-semibold hover:underline"
          >
            Réinitialiser les filtres
          </button>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-4">
          {filteredEvents.map((event) => {
            const config = getCategoryConfig(event.category);
            const attachedDocs = event.attachments
              .map((id) => docMap.get(id))
              .filter(Boolean) as MedicalDocument[];

            return (
              <div
                key={event.id}
                className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200/80 p-5 shadow-xs hover:border-slate-300 transition-all"
              >
                <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-start sm:items-center justify-between gap-2 pb-3 border-b border-slate-100">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <span className={`text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md border ${config.badge}`}>
                      {config.label}
                    </span>
                    <span className="text-xs font-mono font-medium text-slate-400 flex items-center gap-1">
                      <Calendar className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                      {event.event_date}
                    </span>
                  </div>

                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1 self-end sm:self-auto">
                    <button
                      onClick={() => onEditEvent(event)}
                      className="p-1.5 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-100 rounded-lg transition-colors"
                      title="Modifier cet événement"
                    >
                      <Edit2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                    </button>
                    <button
                      onClick={() => {
                        if (window.confirm(`Supprimer l'événement "${event.title}" ?`)) {
                          onDeleteEvent(event.id);
                        }
                      }}
                      className="p-1.5 text-slate-400 hover:text-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-50 rounded-lg transition-colors"
                      title="Supprimer cet événement"
                    >
                      <Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Event Main Content */}
                <div className="mt-3">
                  <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">{event.title}</h3>
                  <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-teal-800 mt-0.5 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                    <Stethoscope className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-teal-600" />
                    {event.doctor_or_facility}
                  </p>
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-600 mt-2 leading-relaxed">
                    {event.description}
                  </p>
                </div>

                {/* Treatment Notes */}
                {event.treatment_notes && (
                  <div className="mt-3 p-3 bg-purple-50/70 border border-purple-100 rounded-xl text-xs text-purple-900 flex items-start gap-2">
                    <Pill className="w-4 h-4 text-purple-600 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
                    <div>
                      <strong className="font-semibold">Recommandations / Prescription :</strong>{' '}
                      <span>{event.treatment_notes}</span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}

                {/* Vital Signs (if present) */}
                {event.vital_signs && (
                  <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 pt-2 text-xs text-slate-500">
                    {event.vital_signs.bp && (
                      <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 font-mono bg-slate-50 px-2 py-1 rounded-md border border-slate-200">
                        <Activity className="w-3 h-3 text-teal-600" />
                        Tension: <strong>{event.vital_signs.bp} mmHg</strong>
                      </span>
                    )}
                    {event.vital_signs.hr && (
                      <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 font-mono bg-slate-50 px-2 py-1 rounded-md border border-slate-200">
                        <Heart className="w-3 h-3 text-rose-500" />
                        Pouls: <strong>{event.vital_signs.hr} bpm</strong>
                      </span>
                    )}
                    {event.vital_signs.temp_c && (
                      <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 font-mono bg-slate-50 px-2 py-1 rounded-md border border-slate-200">
                        Temp: <strong>{event.vital_signs.temp_c} °C</strong>
                      </span>
                    )}
                  </div>
                )}

                {/* Attached Documents */}
                {attachedDocs.length > 0 && (
                  <div className="mt-3 pt-3 border-t border-slate-100">
                    <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider flex items-center gap-1 mb-1.5">
                      <Paperclip className="w-3 h-3" />
                      Documents joints du Coffre-fort ({attachedDocs.length})
                    </span>
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                      {attachedDocs.map((doc) => (
                        <button
                          key={doc.id}
                          onClick={() => onPreviewDocument(doc)}
                          className="px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-teal-50 hover:bg-teal-100/80 text-teal-800 border border-teal-200 text-xs font-semibold flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-colors"
                        >
                          <FileText className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-teal-600" />
                          <span className="truncate max-w-[200px]">{doc.title}</span>
                          <span className="text-[10px] text-teal-600 bg-white/70 px-1 rounded">
                            Aperçu
                          </span>
                        </button>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};


========== FILE: ./src/components/views/SharingView.tsx ==========

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import {
  QrCode,
  ShieldCheck,
  ShieldAlert,
  Clock,
  UserCheck,
  Calendar,
  FileText,
  AlertTriangle,
  Activity,
  PlusCircle,
  CheckCircle2,
  Lock,
  ExternalLink,
  ChevronRight,
  Flame,
} from 'lucide-react';
import {
  HealthEvent,
  MedicalDocument,
  SharingSession,
  SharingScope,
  PatientProfile,
} from '../../types';
import { QRCodeDisplay } from '../common/QRCodeDisplay';

interface SharingViewProps {
  patient: PatientProfile;
  events: HealthEvent[];
  documents: MedicalDocument[];
  sessions: SharingSession[];
  onCreateSession: (
    recipient: string,
    purpose: string,
    durationMinutes: number,
    oneTimeUse: boolean,
    scope: SharingScope
  ) => void;
  onRevokeSession: (sessionId: string) => void;
  onOpenDoctorPortal: (token: string) => void;
}

export const SharingView: React.FC<SharingViewProps> = ({
  patient,
  events,
  documents,
  sessions,
  onCreateSession,
  onRevokeSession,
  onOpenDoctorPortal,
}) => {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<'create' | 'list'>('create');

  // Form states for creating session
  const [recipient, setRecipient] = useState('Dr. Omar Bennani (Cardiologie)');
  const [purpose, setPurpose] = useState('Consultation médicale et avis spécialisé');
  const [durationMinutes, setDurationMinutes] = useState<number>(60); // 1 hour
  const [oneTimeUse, setOneTimeUse] = useState<boolean>(false);

  // Granular consent state
  const [includeAllergies, setIncludeAllergies] = useState(true);
  const [includeVitals, setIncludeVitals] = useState(true);
  const [includeEmergency, setIncludeEmergency] = useState(true);
  const [allEvents, setAllEvents] = useState(false);
  const [selectedEventIds, setSelectedEventIds] = useState<string[]>(
    events.slice(0, 2).map((e) => e.id)
  );
  const [allDocs, setAllDocs] = useState(false);
  const [selectedDocIds, setSelectedDocIds] = useState<string[]>(
    documents.slice(0, 2).map((d) => d.id)
  );

  const [createdSessionId, setCreatedSessionId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const handleCreate = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!recipient.trim()) return;

    const scope: SharingScope = {
      all_events: allEvents,
      event_ids: allEvents ? [] : selectedEventIds,
      all_docs: allDocs,
      doc_ids: allDocs ? [] : selectedDocIds,
      include_allergies: includeAllergies,
      include_vitals: includeVitals,
      include_emergency: includeEmergency,
    };

    onCreateSession(
      recipient.trim(),
      purpose.trim() || 'Consultation de santé',
      durationMinutes,
      oneTimeUse,
      scope
    );

    setActiveTab('list');
  };

  const toggleEventId = (id: string) => {
    setSelectedEventIds((prev) =>
      prev.includes(id) ? prev.filter((item) => item !== id) : [...prev, id]
    );
  };

  const toggleDocId = (id: string) => {
    setSelectedDocIds((prev) =>
      prev.includes(id) ? prev.filter((item) => item !== id) : [...prev, id]
    );
  };

  const activeSessions = sessions.filter(
    (s) => !s.is_revoked && new Date(s.expires_at).getTime() > Date.now()
  );

  const expiredOrRevokedSessions = sessions.filter(
    (s) => s.is_revoked || new Date(s.expires_at).getTime() <= Date.now()
  );

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-start sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <h1 className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 tracking-tight">
              Partage Sécurisé & Consentement Patient
            </h1>
            <span className="text-[10px] font-bold bg-teal-50 text-teal-700 border border-teal-200 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full flex items-center gap-1">
              <ShieldCheck className="w-3 h-3" /> Zéro Fuite de Données
            </span>
          </div>
          <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-500 mt-0.5">
            Générez des QR codes temporaires et choisissez au cas par cas ce que le praticien peut consulter
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Tab switch */}
        <div className="flex items-center bg-slate-100 p-1 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold">
          <button
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('create')}
            className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg transition-all ${
              activeTab === 'create'
                ? 'bg-white text-slate-900 shadow-2xs font-bold'
                : 'text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900'
            }`}
          >
            Nouveau Partage
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('list')}
            className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg transition-all flex items-center gap-1.5 ${
              activeTab === 'list'
                ? 'bg-white text-slate-900 shadow-2xs font-bold'
                : 'text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900'
            }`}
          >
            <span>Sessions en cours</span>
            {activeSessions.length > 0 && (
              <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-emerald-500 animate-pulse" />
            )}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {activeTab === 'create' ? (
        /* Consent & Sharing Builder Wizard */
        <form onSubmit={handleCreate} className="space-y-6">
          {/* Step 1: Destination & Expiration */}
          <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200/80 p-6 shadow-xs space-y-4">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 pb-3 border-b border-slate-100">
              <div className="w-7 h-7 rounded-lg bg-teal-600 text-white font-bold text-xs flex items-center justify-center">
                1
              </div>
              <div>
                <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900">Destinataire et Durée de Validité</h3>
                <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">À qui confiez-vous l'accès et pour combien de temps ?</p>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                  Médecin ou Établissement destinataire <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  placeholder="Ex: Dr. Omar Bennani (Cardiologie), Clinique Agdal..."
                  value={recipient}
                  onChange={(e) => setRecipient(e.target.value)}
                  required
                  className="w-full text-xs rounded-xl border border-slate-300 p-2.5 focus:border-teal-500 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-teal-500"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                  Motif ou Contexte de consultation
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  placeholder="Ex: Consultation cardiologique, bilan pré-opératoire..."
                  value={purpose}
                  onChange={(e) => setPurpose(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full text-xs rounded-xl border border-slate-300 p-2.5 focus:border-teal-500 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-teal-500"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Expiration Presets */}
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-2 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <Clock className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-teal-600" />
                Durée de validité du QR code (Auto-révocation)
              </label>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-4 gap-2.5">
                {[
                  { mins: 15, label: '15 minutes', desc: 'Consultation rapide' },
                  { mins: 60, label: '1 heure', desc: 'Examen approfondi' },
                  { mins: 1440, label: '24 heures', desc: 'Hospitalisation de jour' },
                  { mins: 10080, label: '7 jours', desc: 'Suivi post-opératoire' },
                ].map((preset) => {
                  const isSelected = durationMinutes === preset.mins;
                  return (
                    <button
                      key={preset.mins}
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => setDurationMinutes(preset.mins)}
                      className={`p-3 rounded-xl border text-left transition-all ${
                        isSelected
                          ? 'border-teal-600 bg-teal-50/60 ring-2 ring-teal-500/20'
                          : 'border-slate-200 hover:border-slate-300 bg-white'
                      }`}
                    >
                      <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-900 block">{preset.label}</span>
                      <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 block mt-0.5">{preset.desc}</span>
                    </button>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* One-time use checkbox */}
            <div className="p-3 bg-slate-50 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 flex items-center justify-between">
              <div>
                <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-800 block">
                  Usage unique (Révocation automatique après consultation)
                </span>
                <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-500">
                  Dès que le médecin a consulté le dossier, le jeton devient immédiatement invalide.
                </span>
              </div>
              <input
                type="checkbox"
                checked={oneTimeUse}
                onChange={(e) => setOneTimeUse(e.target.checked)}
                className="w-4 h-4 rounded text-teal-600 focus:ring-teal-500 cursor-pointer"
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Step 2: Granular Consent Matrix */}
          <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200/80 p-6 shadow-xs space-y-5">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 pb-3 border-b border-slate-100">
              <div className="w-7 h-7 rounded-lg bg-teal-600 text-white font-bold text-xs flex items-center justify-center">
                2
              </div>
              <div>
                <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900">Périmètre du Consentement Médical</h3>
                <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">
                  Cochez uniquement les informations médicales que vous autorisez à transmettre
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Critical Info Toggles */}
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3">
              <label className="p-3.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 hover:border-teal-300 bg-white flex items-start gap-2.5 cursor-pointer">
                <input
                  type="checkbox"
                  checked={includeAllergies}
                  onChange={(e) => setIncludeAllergies(e.target.checked)}
                  className="rounded text-teal-600 focus:ring-teal-500 mt-0.5"
                />
                <div>
                  <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-1">
                    <AlertTriangle className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-500" />
                    Allergies & Alertes
                  </span>
                  <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 mt-0.5">
                    Transmettre le signalement de votre allergie à la pénicilline
                  </p>
                </div>
              </label>

              <label className="p-3.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 hover:border-teal-300 bg-white flex items-start gap-2.5 cursor-pointer">
                <input
                  type="checkbox"
                  checked={includeVitals}
                  onChange={(e) => setIncludeVitals(e.target.checked)}
                  className="rounded text-teal-600 focus:ring-teal-500 mt-0.5"
                />
                <div>
                  <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-1">
                    <Activity className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-teal-600" />
                    Constantes Vitales
                  </span>
                  <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 mt-0.5">
                    Dernières mesures (Tension 120/80, Pouls 72 bpm)
                  </p>
                </div>
              </label>

              <label className="p-3.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 hover:border-teal-300 bg-white flex items-start gap-2.5 cursor-pointer">
                <input
                  type="checkbox"
                  checked={includeEmergency}
                  onChange={(e) => setIncludeEmergency(e.target.checked)}
                  className="rounded text-teal-600 focus:ring-teal-500 mt-0.5"
                />
                <div>
                  <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-1">
                    <UserCheck className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-blue-600" />
                    Contact d'Urgence
                  </span>
                  <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 mt-0.5">
                    Coordonnées de Mme Amina Mansouri (Épouse)
                  </p>
                </div>
              </label>
            </div>

            {/* Granular Events Selector */}
            <div className="space-y-2 pt-2">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-800 uppercase tracking-wide">
                  Événements du Carnet de Santé ({allEvents ? 'Tous' : selectedEventIds.length} sélectionné{selectedEventIds.length > 1 ? 's' : ''})
                </span>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setAllEvents(!allEvents)}
                  className="text-xs font-semibold text-teal-600 hover:underline"
                >
                  {allEvents ? 'Choisir au cas par cas' : 'Partager tout le carnet'}
                </button>
              </div>

              {!allEvents && (
                <div className="max-h-48 overflow-y-auto space-y-1.5 p-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200">
                  {events.map((evt) => {
                    const isChecked = selectedEventIds.includes(evt.id);
                    return (
                      <label
                        key={evt.id}
                        className={`flex items-center gap-3 p-2 rounded-lg text-xs cursor-pointer transition-colors ${
                          isChecked
                            ? 'bg-teal-50/80 border border-teal-200 text-teal-950 font-semibold'
                            : 'hover:bg-white text-slate-700'
                        }`}
                      >
                        <input
                          type="checkbox"
                          checked={isChecked}
                          onChange={() => toggleEventId(evt.id)}
                          className="rounded text-teal-600 focus:ring-teal-500"
                        />
                        <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-slate-400 shrink-0">
                          {evt.event_date}
                        </span>
                        <span className="truncate flex-1">{evt.title}</span>
                        <span className="text-[10px] px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-white text-slate-500 border border-slate-100">
                          {evt.category}
                        </span>
                      </label>
                    );
                  })}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Granular Documents Selector */}
            <div className="space-y-2 pt-2">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-800 uppercase tracking-wide">
                  Documents du Coffre-fort ({allDocs ? 'Tous' : selectedDocIds.length} sélectionné{selectedDocIds.length > 1 ? 's' : ''})
                </span>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setAllDocs(!allDocs)}
                  className="text-xs font-semibold text-teal-600 hover:underline"
                >
                  {allDocs ? 'Choisir au cas par cas' : 'Partager tous les documents'}
                </button>
              </div>

              {!allDocs && (
                <div className="max-h-48 overflow-y-auto space-y-1.5 p-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200">
                  {documents.map((doc) => {
                    const isChecked = selectedDocIds.includes(doc.id);
                    return (
                      <label
                        key={doc.id}
                        className={`flex items-center gap-3 p-2 rounded-lg text-xs cursor-pointer transition-colors ${
                          isChecked
                            ? 'bg-teal-50/80 border border-teal-200 text-teal-950 font-semibold'
                            : 'hover:bg-white text-slate-700'
                        }`}
                      >
                        <input
                          type="checkbox"
                          checked={isChecked}
                          onChange={() => toggleDocId(doc.id)}
                          className="rounded text-teal-600 focus:ring-teal-500"
                        />
                        <FileText className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400 shrink-0" />
                        <span className="truncate flex-1">{doc.title}</span>
                        <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-mono">
                          {doc.doctor_or_lab}
                        </span>
                      </label>
                    );
                  })}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Action Button */}
          <div className="flex justify-end">
            <button
              type="submit"
              className="px-6 py-3 bg-teal-600 hover:bg-teal-700 text-white rounded-xl text-sm font-bold flex items-center gap-2 shadow-md hover:scale-[1.01] transition-all"
            >
              <QrCode className="w-5 h-5" />
              <span>Générer le QR Code Éphémère Sécurisé</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>
      ) : (
        /* Sessions List & Active QR Cards */
        <div className="space-y-6">
          {/* Active Sessions */}
          <div>
            <h2 className="text-sm font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-teal-800 mb-3 flex items-center gap-2">
              <span className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-500 animate-pulse"></span>
              Sessions Actives ({activeSessions.length})
            </h2>

            {activeSessions.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200/80 p-8 text-center">
                <ShieldCheck className="w-8 h-8 text-slate-300 mx-auto mb-2" />
                <h3 className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-700">Aucun accès actif</h3>
                <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 mt-0.5">
                  Aucun médecin n'a actuellement accès à votre dossier.
                </p>
                <button
                  onClick={() => setActiveTab('create')}
                  className="mt-3 px-3.5 py-1.5 bg-teal-600 text-white rounded-lg text-xs font-bold hover:bg-teal-700"
                >
                  Créer un partage
                </button>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
                {activeSessions.map((session) => {
                  const shareUrl = `${window.location.origin}/share/${session.token}`;
                  return (
                    <div
                      key={session.id}
                      className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-teal-200 p-6 shadow-sm flex flex-col justify-between"
                    >
                      <div>
                        <div className="flex items-center justify-between pb-3 border-b border-slate-100 mb-4">
                          <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-900">
                            {session.recipient_label}
                          </span>
                          <span className="text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800">
                            Valide jusqu'à {new Date(session.expires_at).toLocaleTimeString('fr-FR', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' })}
                          </span>
                        </div>

                        <p className="text-xs text-slate-600 mb-4">
                          Motif : <strong>{session.purpose}</strong>
                        </p>

                        {/* QR Code view */}
                        <QRCodeDisplay
                          value={shareUrl}
                          recipientLabel={session.recipient_label}
                          expiresAt={session.expires_at}
                          isRevoked={session.is_revoked}
                          onOpenDoctorView={() => onOpenDoctorPortal(session.token)}
                          onRevoke={() => {
                            if (window.confirm('Révoquer immédiatement l\'accès pour ce praticien ?')) {
                              onRevokeSession(session.id);
                            }
                          }}
                        />

                        {/* Consented summary */}
                        <div className="mt-4 p-3 bg-slate-50 rounded-xl text-xs text-slate-600 space-y-1">
                          <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase block">Données consenties :</span>
                          <p>
                            • {session.scope.all_events ? 'Tous les événements' : `${session.scope.event_ids.length} événement(s) sélectionnés`}
                          </p>
                          <p>
                            • {session.scope.all_docs ? 'Tous les documents' : `${session.scope.doc_ids.length} document(s) sélectionnés`}
                          </p>
                          <p>
                            • Allergies : {session.scope.include_allergies ? 'Oui' : 'Non'} • Constantes : {session.scope.include_vitals ? 'Oui' : 'Non'}
                          </p>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Expired or Revoked History */}
          {expiredOrRevokedSessions.length > 0 && (
            <div className="pt-4">
              <h2 className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400 mb-3">
                Historique des Partages Révoqués ou Expirés ({expiredOrRevokedSessions.length})
              </h2>
              <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200/80 overflow-hidden divide-y divide-slate-100">
                {expiredOrRevokedSessions.map((session) => (
                  <div
                    key={session.id}
                    className="p-4 flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-start sm:items-center justify-between gap-3 text-xs opacity-75 hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity"
                  >
                    <div>
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        <strong className="text-slate-800">{session.recipient_label}</strong>
                        <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-slate-400">
                          {session.token}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                      <p className="text-slate-500 text-[11px] mt-0.5">{session.purpose}</p>
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      <span
                        className={`text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full ${
                          session.is_revoked
                            ? 'bg-rose-100 text-rose-700'
                            : 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-700'
                        }`}
                      >
                        {session.is_revoked ? 'Révoqué par le patient' : 'Expiré'}
                      </span>
                      <button
                        onClick={() => onOpenDoctorPortal(session.token)}
                        className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded border border-slate-200 text-[11px] text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-50"
                      >
                        Tester sécurité
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};


========== FILE: ./src/components/views/VaultView.tsx ==========

import React, { useState, useMemo } from 'react';
import {
  FolderLock,
  UploadCloud,
  Search,
  Filter,
  FileText,
  Eye,
  Download,
  Trash2,
  Calendar,
  Building,
  Tag,
  ShieldCheck,
  FileSpreadsheet,
  FileImage,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { MedicalDocument, DocumentCategory } from '../../types';

interface VaultViewProps {
  documents: MedicalDocument[];
  onOpenUpload: () => void;
  onPreviewDocument: (doc: MedicalDocument) => void;
  onDeleteDocument: (id: string) => void;
}

export const VaultView: React.FC<VaultViewProps> = ({
  documents,
  onOpenUpload,
  onPreviewDocument,
  onDeleteDocument,
}) => {
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [selectedCategory, setSelectedCategory] = useState<string>('ALL');

  const categories: { id: string; label: string }[] = [
    { id: 'ALL', label: 'Tous les documents' },
    { id: 'ORDONNANCE', label: 'Ordonnances' },
    { id: 'BILAN', label: 'Bilans & Biologie' },
    { id: 'IMAGERIE', label: 'Imagerie & Radios' },
    { id: 'COMPTE_RENDU', label: 'Comptes-Rendus' },
    { id: 'CERTIFICAT', label: 'Certificats' },
  ];

  const filteredDocuments = useMemo(() => {
    return documents.filter((doc) => {
      const matchesCategory =
        selectedCategory === 'ALL' || doc.category === selectedCategory;
      const matchesQuery =
        searchQuery.trim() === '' ||
        doc.title.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) ||
        doc.doctor_or_lab.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) ||
        doc.tags.some((t) => t.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()));
      return matchesCategory && matchesQuery;
    });
  }, [documents, selectedCategory, searchQuery]);

  const formatFileSize = (bytes: number) => {
    if (bytes < 1024) return `${bytes} B`;
    if (bytes < 1024 * 1024) return `${(bytes / 1024).toFixed(1)} Ko`;
    return `${(bytes / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(2)} Mo`;
  };

  const getDocIcon = (cat: DocumentCategory) => {
    switch (cat) {
      case 'ORDONNANCE':
        return <FileText className="w-5 h-5 text-teal-600" />;
      case 'BILAN':
        return <FileSpreadsheet className="w-5 h-5 text-emerald-600" />;
      case 'IMAGERIE':
        return <FileImage className="w-5 h-5 text-blue-600" />;
      default:
        return <FileText className="w-5 h-5 text-slate-600" />;
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-start sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <h1 className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 tracking-tight">
              Coffre-fort Médical Sécurisé
            </h1>
            <span className="text-[10px] font-bold bg-teal-50 text-teal-700 border border-teal-200 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full flex items-center gap-1">
              <ShieldCheck className="w-3 h-3" /> Chiffré
            </span>
          </div>
          <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-500 mt-0.5">
            Archivage certifié de vos ordonnances, comptes-rendus et examens médicaux
          </p>
        </div>

        <button
          onClick={onOpenUpload}
          className="px-4 py-2.5 bg-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-800 text-white rounded-xl text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-2 shadow-sm transition-all hover:scale-[1.02]"
        >
          <UploadCloud className="w-4 h-4 text-teal-400" />
          <span>Déposer un document</span>
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Search and Category Filter */}
      <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200/80 shadow-xs space-y-3">
        <div className="relative">
          <Search className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2" />
          <input
            type="text"
            placeholder="Rechercher par titre, laboratoire, médecin, tag..."
            value={searchQuery}
            onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
            className="w-full text-xs rounded-xl border border-slate-200 pl-10 pr-4 py-2.5 bg-slate-50/50 focus:bg-white focus:border-teal-500 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-teal-500 transition-all"
          />
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 overflow-x-auto pb-1 scrollbar-none">
          {categories.map((cat) => {
            const isSelected = selectedCategory === cat.id;
            return (
              <button
                key={cat.id}
                onClick={() => setSelectedCategory(cat.id)}
                className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold whitespace-nowrap transition-all ${
                  isSelected
                    ? 'bg-teal-700 text-white shadow-2xs'
                    : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-200/70 hover:text-slate-900'
                }`}
              >
                {cat.label}
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Documents Grid */}
      {filteredDocuments.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200/80 p-12 text-center">
          <FolderLock className="w-10 h-10 text-slate-300 mx-auto mb-3" />
          <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-700">Aucun document dans le coffre-fort</h3>
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-1 max-w-sm mx-auto">
            Aucun document ne correspond à vos filtres. Vous pouvez déposer votre première ordonnance ou analyse.
          </p>
          <button
            onClick={onOpenUpload}
            className="mt-4 px-4 py-2 bg-teal-600 text-white rounded-xl text-xs font-bold hover:bg-teal-700 transition-colors"
          >
            Déposer un document maintenant
          </button>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
          {filteredDocuments.map((doc) => {
            return (
              <div
                key={doc.id}
                className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200/80 p-5 shadow-xs hover:shadow-md hover:border-slate-300 transition-all flex flex-col justify-between"
              >
                <div>
                  <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3 mb-3">
                    <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-100 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
                      {getDocIcon(doc.category)}
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
                      <span className="text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 uppercase">
                        {doc.category}
                      </span>
                      <button
                        onClick={() => {
                          if (window.confirm(`Supprimer le document "${doc.title}" du coffre-fort ?`)) {
                            onDeleteDocument(doc.id);
                          }
                        }}
                        className="p-1 text-slate-400 hover:text-rose-600 rounded"
                        title="Supprimer du coffre-fort"
                      >
                        <Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 line-clamp-1">{doc.title}</h3>
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 flex items-center gap-1.5 mt-1">
                    <Building className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400 shrink-0" />
                    <span className="truncate">{doc.doctor_or_lab}</span>
                  </p>

                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3 text-xs text-slate-400 mt-2 font-mono">
                    <span className="flex items-center gap-1">
                      <Calendar className="w-3 h-3" />
                      {doc.issue_date}
                    </span>
                    <span>•</span>
                    <span>{formatFileSize(doc.file_size_bytes)}</span>
                  </div>

                  {doc.tags.length > 0 && (
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1 mt-3 flex-wrap">
                      <Tag className="w-3 h-3 text-slate-400" />
                      {doc.tags.map((t, idx) => (
                        <span
                          key={idx}
                          className="px-1.5 py-0.5 bg-slate-50 text-slate-600 rounded text-[10px] font-medium"
                        >
                          #{t}
                        </span>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>

                <div className="pt-4 border-t border-slate-100 mt-4 flex items-center gap-2">
                  <button
                    onClick={() => onPreviewDocument(doc)}
                    className="flex-1 py-2 px-3 bg-teal-50 hover:bg-teal-100 text-teal-800 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 transition-colors"
                  >
                    <Eye className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-teal-600" />
                    <span>Consulter aperçu</span>
                  </button>
                  <button
                    onClick={() => {
                      const element = window.document.createElement('a');
                      const file = new Blob([doc.content_preview || 'Document CARNI'], { type: 'text/plain' });
                      element.href = URL.createObjectURL(file);
                      element.download = doc.file_name;
                      window.document.body.appendChild(element);
                      element.click();
                      window.document.body.removeChild(element);
                    }}
                    className="p-2 border border-slate-200 text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100 rounded-xl transition-colors"
                    title="Télécharger"
                  >
                    <Download className="w-4 h-4" />
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

========== FILE: ./src/services/api.ts ==========

import {
  PatientProfile,
  HealthEvent,
  MedicalDocument,
  SharingSession,
  AuditLogEntry,
  SharingScope,
} from '../types';

// In development, target the Axum Rust server directly at 127.0.0.1:3000 with CORS
const API_BASE =
  window.location.port === '5173'
    ? 'http://127.0.0.1:3000/api'
    : '/api';


export const api = {
  async checkHealth(): Promise<boolean> {
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/health`, { method: 'GET' });
      return res.ok;
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  },

  async getPatient(): Promise<PatientProfile> {
    const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/patient`);
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Impossible de charger le profil patient');
    return res.json();
  },

  async getEvents(): Promise<HealthEvent[]> {
    const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/events`);
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Impossible de charger les événements');
    return res.json();
  },

  async createEvent(event: Omit<HealthEvent, 'id' | 'created_at'>): Promise<HealthEvent> {
    const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/events`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify(event),
    });
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Erreur lors de la création de l\'événement');
    return res.json();
  },

  async updateEvent(id: string, event: Partial<HealthEvent>): Promise<HealthEvent> {
    const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/events/${id}`, {
      method: 'PUT',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify(event),
    });
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Erreur lors de la mise à jour de l\'événement');
    return res.json();
  },

  async deleteEvent(id: string): Promise<void> {
    const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/events/${id}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Erreur lors de la suppression de l\'événement');
  },

  async getDocuments(): Promise<MedicalDocument[]> {
    const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/documents`);
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Impossible de charger les documents');
    return res.json();
  },

  async createDocument(doc: Omit<MedicalDocument, 'id' | 'created_at'>): Promise<MedicalDocument> {
    const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/documents`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify(doc),
    });
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Erreur lors du dépôt du document');
    return res.json();
  },

  async deleteDocument(id: string): Promise<void> {
    const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/documents/${id}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Erreur lors de la suppression du document');
  },

  async getSharingSessions(): Promise<SharingSession[]> {
    const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/shares`);
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Impossible de charger les sessions');
    return res.json();
  },

  async createSharingSession(data: {
    recipient_label: string;
    purpose: string;
    duration_minutes: number;
    one_time_use: boolean;
    scope: SharingScope;
  }): Promise<SharingSession> {
    const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/shares`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify(data),
    });
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Erreur lors de la création du partage');
    return res.json();
  },

  async revokeSharingSession(id: string): Promise<void> {
    const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/shares/${id}/revoke`, { method: 'POST' });
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Erreur lors de la révocation du partage');
  },

  async accessSharedSession(token: string): Promise<{
    session: SharingSession;
    patient: Partial<PatientProfile>;
    events: HealthEvent[];
    documents: MedicalDocument[];
  }> {
    const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/shares/access/${token}`);
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Jeton de consultation invalide, expiré ou révoqué');
    return res.json();
  },

  async getAuditLogs(): Promise<AuditLogEntry[]> {
    const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/audit`);
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Impossible de charger le journal d\'audit');
    return res.json();
  },
};
